### [1] [Stochastic Saddle Avoidance Beyond Unit Excitation and Smoothness: A Pathwise Lyapunov-Perron Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03001)
*Junwen Qiu,Bohao Ma,Andre Milzarek,Junyu Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在没有单位激励（UE）假设下，随机递归几乎必然避开严格鞍点的抽象定理，用可验证的路径条件替代UE要求，并应用于随机镜像下降（含SGD）、随机重排和近端随机梯度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单位激励（UE）假设要求随机误差在每个方向上都有均匀正分量，这虽然能直接排除收敛到严格鞍点，但与许多实际随机优化场景不符：过参数化或插值模型中噪声在驻点附近可能消失；有限和问题中随机梯度噪声可能位于低维数据相关子空间。因此需要不依赖UE的严格鞍点避障理论。

Method: 证明了一个无需UE的抽象几乎必然避障定理，用可验证的路径条件替代UE要求。由于随机采样的映射通常不共享不动点，经典的确定性中心稳定流形论证无法直接适用，因此采用路径依赖的变量替换结合路径化Lyapunov-Perron证明策略。

Result: 作为应用，获得了随机镜像下降（包括SGD）和随机重排的严格鞍点避障结果；对非光滑复合目标，证明了近端型随机梯度方法的避障结果。结合相应的迭代收敛保证，可建立到原目标函数局部极小值的收敛性。

Conclusion: 通过用可验证的路径条件替代UE假设，建立了一个更通用、更贴合实际噪声结构的严格鞍点避障理论框架，覆盖了过参数化、插值模型和有限和问题等常见但UE不成立的情形。

Abstract: Unit excitation (UE) is a common assumption in stochastic saddle avoidance: the stochastic error must have a uniformly positive component along every direction, in expectation. This condition gives a direct way to rule out convergence to strict saddles, but it also oversimplifies the actual noise structure, and does not match many stochastic optimization regimes. In overparameterized or interpolation models, the noise may vanish near stationarity. In finite-sum problems, the stochastic gradient noise may lie in a low-dimensional, data-dependent subspace. In these (common) scenarios, UE is naturally not satisfied. In this paper, we prove an abstract almost sure avoidance theorem for stochastic recursions without UE. The theorem replaces UE-type requirements by verifiable pathwise conditions. In applications, these conditions follow, e.g., from local smoothness and finite-moment assumptions under standard i.i.d. sampling, or from the finite-sum structure under without-replacement sampling. Since the stochastically sampled maps generally do not share a fixed point, the celebrated center-stable manifold argument used in deterministic analyses is not directly applicable. Instead, we use a path-dependent change of variables together with a pathwise Lyapunov--Perron-based proof strategy. As applications, we obtain strict saddle avoidance for stochastic mirror descent (including SGD) and for random reshuffling. For nonsmooth composite objectives, we prove avoidance results for a proximal-type stochastic gradient method. Combining these insights with suitable iterate convergence guarantees, this allows establishing convergence to local minimizers of the original objective function.

</details>


### [2] [Oracle-Based Distributionally Robust Optimization under Optimal Transport Ambiguity Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03075)
*Guixian Chen,Salar Fattahi,Soroosh Shafiee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的求解最优传输模糊集分布鲁棒优化（DRO）问题的新算法。核心洞察是将内层最坏情况期望问题精确约简为标量预算分配任务，避免传统大规模提升重构带来的计算负担。算法支持在至多N+1个点上恢复最优最坏情况分布，并嵌入基于oracle的分布最优响应框架直接计算近似原始-对偶解。同时扩展到对偶DRO形式，证明了存在最多min{N+n+1, KN}个原子的最坏情况分布，并提供高效的凸规划提取方法。数值实验表明该方法显著优于基于重构的现有求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DRO问题通过将极小极大问题重构为单层凸规划来求解，虽然理论上可处理，但引入大量辅助变量和严格的锥约束，实际计算规模很差。作者旨在解决这一可扩展性瓶颈，设计更高效的算法。

Method: 1) 将内层最坏情况期望问题精确约简为标量预算分配任务，绕过大规模提升重构。2) 提出快速后处理方案，恢复支撑在至多N+1个点上的最优最坏情况分布。3) 嵌入oracle-based分布最优响应框架，计算近似原始-对偶解。4) 扩展到对偶DRO，证明存在支撑在至多min{N+n+1, KN}个原子的最坏情况分布，并通过凸规划约简提取。

Result: 数值实验表明所提方法在计算效率上显著优于基于重构的先进求解器，同时保留了对最优最坏情况分布的恢复能力和原始-对偶解的近似能力。

Conclusion: 通过标量预算分配的精确约简，成功实现了DRO问题的高效求解，克服了传统重构方法的规模瓶颈，并在原始与对偶两个层面都提供了理论保证和实用算法。

Abstract: Distributionally robust optimization (DRO) with optimal transport ambiguity sets is traditionally solved by reformulating the minimax problem into a single-level convex program. While theoretically tractable, these reformulations introduce numerous auxiliary variables and demanding conic constraints that scale poorly in practice. In this paper, we address this challenge by reducing the inner worst-case expectation problem exactly to a scalar budget allocation task. This structural insight yields an efficient algorithm that bypasses large lifted reformulations, alongside a fast post-processing scheme to recover an optimal worst-case distribution supported on at most $N+1$ points, where $N$ denotes the sample size. We embed this procedure within an oracle-based distributional best-response framework to directly compute an approximate primal-dual solution to the overall DRO problem. Furthermore, we extend our analysis to the dual DRO formulation, proving the existence of a least-favorable distribution supported on at most $\min\{N+n+1, KN\}$ atoms, where $n$ and $K$ denote the decision dimension and number of loss components, respectively, and provide an efficient convex programming reduction to extract it from the solution of the primal DRO. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art reformulation-based solvers.

</details>


### [3] [Optimal Nonergodic Primal-Dual Complexity of Efficient Inexact Parameter-Free Augmented Lagrangian Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03170)
*Arnesh Sujanani,Saeed Ghadimi,Henry Wołkowicz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为线性约束凸复合优化问题提出了三种非精确增广拉格朗日（AL）方法，针对可验证的近似KKT解而非仅基于目标值误差的精度保证。在凸情形下实现了最优的原始-对偶复杂度O(ε⁻¹)，在强凸情形下达到近最优复杂度O(ε⁻¹ᐟ²log(ε⁻¹))，并在数值实验中相对PAL方法取得5-50倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增广拉格朗日方法在凸优化中的复杂度保证不够理想（如O(ε⁻⁴ᐟ³)、O(ε⁻⁷ᐟ⁴)、O(ε⁻²)），而PAL方法的O(ε⁻¹log(ε⁻¹))复杂度也并非最优。同时，现有方法多以目标值误差作为精度度量，而非可验证的近似KKT解，这在实际应用中缺乏可操作性和保证性。

Method: 提出了三种非精确增广拉格朗日方法，适用于目标为光滑凸函数与非光滑闭真凸函数（紧域）之和的线性约束凸复合优化问题。其中两种变体无需参数调整（parameter-free），并建立了非遍历（non-ergodic）收敛保证，其中一种变体还获得了更强的最后迭代（last-iterate）保证。

Result: 凸情形下三种方案的原始-对偶复杂度为最优的O(ε⁻¹)，超越先前的O(ε⁻⁴ᐟ³)、O(ε⁻⁷ᐟ⁴)、O(ε⁻²)以及PAL的O(ε⁻¹log(ε⁻¹))。强凸情形下达到近最优复杂度O(ε⁻¹ᐟ²log(ε⁻¹))。在弹性网络最小二乘回归、组稀疏Huber化SVM及量子半定规划等六类问题上，相比PAL方法获得5-50倍的加速。

Conclusion: 所提出的非精确增广拉格朗日方法在理论和实践两方面均显著优于先前方法：理论上实现了最优或近最优的复杂度，提供可验证的近似KKT精度保证及非遍历收敛；实践上在广泛的重要问题类别上展示了显著的加速优势。

Abstract: We develop inexact augmented Lagrangian (AL) methods for linearly constrained convex composite optimization problems whose objective is the sum of a smooth convex function and a possibly nonsmooth closed proper convex function with compact domain. Unlike primal accuracy guarantees based on objective-value error, our methods target verifiable approximate KKT solutions. In the convex setting, we propose three inexact AL schemes with optimal primal-dual complexity $\mathcal O(ε^{-1})$, improving prior AL bounds such as $\mathcal O(ε^{-4/3})$, $\mathcal O(ε^{-7/4})$, and $\mathcal O(ε^{-2})$, and improving the $\mathcal O(ε^{-1}\log(ε^{-1}))$ guarantees of proximal augmented Lagrangian (PAL) methods. Two of the three convex variants are parameter-free. We also establish non-ergodic convergence guarantees, including a stronger last-iterate guarantee for one variant. In the strongly convex setting, our methods achieve near-optimal complexity $\mathcal O(ε^{-1/2}\log(ε^{-1}))$, with two parameter-free variants. Numerical experiments on six important problem classes, including elastic-net least-squares regression, group-sparse Huberized support vector machines, and a quantum semidefinite program (SDP), demonstrate substantial computational advantages of our inexact AL methods over a representative PAL method, with speedups frequently ranging from $5$ to $50$ times.

</details>


### [4] [Optimal Deterministic Oracle Complexity for Weakly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03246)
*Jiajin Li,Siyu Pan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在ρ-弱凸且G-Lipschitz函数上寻找ε-驻点的确定性一阶算法的最优预言机复杂度，证明了下界为Ω(ρG²Δ/ε⁴)，与已知的最优上界匹配，确立了最优确定性预言机复杂度，并揭示了光滑非凸与光滑弱凸优化之间存在本质的ε⁻²复杂度差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于弱凸非光滑优化的下界结果与上界之间存在差距，需要确定该问题的最优确定性预言机复杂度，并理解非光滑几何对复杂度的根本影响。

Method: 通过构造特定实例，利用对抗性的预言机设计，对任意确定性一阶算法（返回函数值和全次微分）进行下界分析，证明在Δ≤G²/ρ条件下需要Ω(ρG²Δ/ε⁴)次查询。

Result: 证明确定性一阶算法在ρ-弱凸、G-Lipschitz非光滑函数上找到ε-驻点需要Ω(ρG²Δ/ε⁴)次查询，该下界与已知最优确定性及随机一阶上界匹配（仅差通用常数）。

Conclusion: 确立了ρ-弱凸非光滑优化中确定性一阶预言的复杂度为Θ(ρG²Δ/ε⁴)，其中ε⁻⁴因子中的额外ε⁻²来源于非光滑几何而非随机性，与光滑非凸优化的Θ(ε⁻²)复杂度形成本质分离。

Abstract: We study the oracle complexity of finding $ε$-stationary points of $ρ$-weakly convex and $G$-Lipschitz functions, where stationarity is measured by the gradient of the Moreau envelope. We consider a first-order oracle that returns both the function value and the full subdifferential at every query point. We prove that every deterministic first-order algorithm requires $ Ω({ρG^2Δ}/{ε^4})$ oracle queries whenever $Δ\leq {G^2}/ρ$, where $f(\bz)-\inf f \leq Δ$. This lower bound matches the best known deterministic and stochastic first-order upper bounds, up to universal constants, and establishes the optimal deterministic oracle complexity. The result reveals a fundamental complexity separation between smooth nonconvex and nonsmooth weakly convex optimization. While smooth nonconvex minimization admits a $Θ(ε^{-2})$ oracle complexity, nonsmooth weakly convex optimization incurs an intrinsic additional $ε^{-2}$ factor arising from nonsmooth geometry rather than stochasticity.

</details>


### [5] [Joint-Range Inequalities for Nonconvex QCQPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03318)
*Liding Xu,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"投影-提升"（project-then-lift）方法的割平面技术，用于求解非凸二次约束二次规划（QCQP）。通过将两个基有效不等式投影到二维联合值域集合中，并给出该联合值域在非凸情形下的闭式凸包描述及凸情形下的半定表示，从而产生一类新的联合值域不等式，可提升回原扩展QCQP松弛。该方法可保持稀疏性，且初步几何实验表明其能显著缩小RLT投影松弛的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸QCQP是广泛存在于优化领域的困难问题，现有的割平面方法（如MIR不等式）主要针对混合整数线性结构，难以有效处理非凸二次项。作者希望通过借鉴MIR的思路，在扩展QCQP松弛中构造更紧的割平面，以更好地逼近非凸可行域，提高求解效率。

Method: 采用"投影-提升"框架：给定扩展QCQP表述中两个基有效不等式，将其相关的两行松弛投影为二维集合，分析两个基不等式中二次函数的联合值域。对非凸联合值域给出闭式凸包描述；对凸联合值域给出半定表示。由此得到联合值域不等式并提升回扩展QCQP。进一步提出secant混合联合值域不等式以更好地处理含连续变量或整数变量线性组合的"混合"项。该方法利用基不等式的支撑集控制提升后不等式的支撑集，保持稀疏性。

Result: 在初步几何实验中，联合值域不等式对通过reformulation-linearization-technique（RLT）构造的投影松弛产生了显著的面积缩减，验证了所提割平面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该联合值域割平面方法为求解非凸QCQP提供了一条新路径，兼具理论上的闭式/半定表示与计算上的稀疏性优势。未来可进一步研究其在一般QCQP求解器中的集成策略及数值性能。

Abstract: We study cutting planes for nonconvex quadratically constrained quadratic programs (QCQPs) through a project-then-lift approach inspired by mixed-integer rounding (MIR) inequalities. Given two base valid inequalities for the extended QCQP formulation, we project the associated two-row relaxation into a two-dimensional set and analyze the joint range of quadratic functions in two base inequalities. For the nonconvex joint range, we give a closed-form convex hull description of the projected set; for the convex joint range, we give its semidefinite representation. This yields a new family of joint-range inequalities, which can be lifted back to the extended QCQP formulation. MIR inequalities can handle ``mixed'' terms: continuous variables or fractional linear combinations of integer variables. Similarly, we propose more flexible secant mixed-joint-range inequalities, which better expose and exploit the nonconvex joint range. The proposed approach preserves sparsity, since the support of each lifted inequality is controlled by that of the base inequalities. In preliminary geometric experiments, the joint-range inequalities yield substantial area reduction of the projected relaxation constructed via reformulation-linearization-technique.

</details>


### [6] [Dynamic Proximal Point Method for Unconstrained Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03349)
*Enrico Bertolazzi,Alberto De Marchi,Davide Stocco*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于无约束优化的新型动态近端点算法，使用自适应更新的对角加权矩阵构造一系列近端子问题，内层用牛顿法与线搜索求解，外层依据内层求解器性能调整参考点与正则化参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近端点算法是求解无约束优化的重要工具，但其性能高度依赖于正则化参数和加权矩阵的选择。现有方法通常采用固定或简单更新的策略，难以在迭代过程中自适应调整，限制了收敛速度和鲁棒性。本文旨在设计一种能够在每次迭代中动态更新对角加权矩阵的近端算法，以提升整体优化效率。

Method: 提出双层结构的动态近端点算法：外层生成由对角加权矩阵正则化的近端子问题，矩阵基于导数信息自适应构建；内层采用结合线搜索的牛顿法求解子问题，以保证全局收敛性。推导了牛顿步的约化线性系统，定义了相应的价值函数，并给出了对角缩放矩阵的实用构造方法及实现伪代码和停止准则。

Result: 论文推导了牛顿步的约化线性系统，定义了价值函数，提出了从导数信息构造对角缩放矩阵的实用方法，并提供了面向实现的伪代码及与算法一致的停止准则，建立了内层牛顿法的全局收敛机制。

Conclusion: 该工作构建了一个完整的动态近端点优化框架，通过自适应对角正则化与内层牛顿法的结合，兼顾了算法的实用性、收敛性和可实现性，为无约束优化提供了新的求解思路。

Abstract: In this work, we present a novel dynamic proximal point algorithm for unconstrained optimization. The method generates a sequence of proximal subproblems, where the quadratic regularization term is weighted by a diagonal matrix that is updated adaptively at each iteration. Each subproblem is solved using an inner Newton's method combined with a line search, which provides a global convergence mechanism for the nonlinear solver. At the outer level, the algorithm updates the reference point and adjusts the regularization parameter based on the performance of the inner Newton solver. We derive the reduced linear system used to compute the Newton step, define the corresponding merit function, and discuss practical approaches for constructing the diagonal scaling matrix from derivative information. The paper also provides implementation-oriented pseudocode and stopping criteria that are consistent with the proposed method.

</details>


### [7] [Fast and slow mean-field games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03376)
*Roxana Dumitrescu,Julian Gutierrez Pineda,Peter Tankov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双时间尺度结构的框架，用于在带共同噪声的平均场博弈（MFG）中构造近似纳什均衡。通过利用快变量的遍历动力学和慢变量的受控/停止过程，构建了一个无共同噪声的"有效"MFG来近似完整MFG的解，并显式构造了ε-MFG均衡，其中时间尺度分离参数控制纳什均衡条件的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带共同噪声的均场博弈通常难以直接求解，因为共同噪声引入了额外的随机性维度，使得均衡分析的复杂度显著增加。现有方法在处理这类问题时往往缺乏系统性的近似框架。本文试图通过双时间尺度结构来简化这一难题，为求解带共同噪声的MFG提供一种高效的近似途径。

Method: 本文采用双时间尺度方法：共同噪声由快变量携带（在遍历动力学下演化），慢变量的漂移系数受快变量影响且被最优控制或停止。关键思想是用一个无共同噪声的"有效"MFG（系数相对于快尺度过程的平稳测度进行平均）来近似完整MFG的纳什均衡。具体地，从有效MFG的均衡出发，通过随机化控制和停止构造完整MFG的显式ε-MFG均衡。此外，本文还获得了带随机化停止的MFG均衡存在性的新结果，并依赖于两尺度扩散的强收敛结果，证明了时间尺度分离参数控制了纳什均衡条件中的误差。

Result: 本文成功构造了显式的ε-MFG均衡，其中近似误差由时间尺度分离参数控制。获得了带随机化停止的MFG均衡存在性的新理论结果，为所提出的近似框架提供了严格的数学基础。数值上通过两尺度扩散的强收敛性保证了近似方案的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个通用且严格的理论框架，将带共同噪声的复杂MFG问题简化为无共同噪声的有效MFG问题，并证明近似误差可控。这一结果不仅提供了求解此类问题的实用途径，还拓展了MFG均衡存在性理论（特别是随机化停止情形），对MFG领域具有重要的理论和方法论贡献。

Abstract: We propose a framework for constructing approximate Nash equilibria in mean-field games (MFG) with common noise based on a two-time-scale structure. In our model, the common noise is carried by a fast variable evolving under ergodic dynamics, while the slow variable is either optimally controlled or stopped. The fast variable enters the dynamics of the slow one through the drift coefficient. The key idea is to construct an approximation to the solution of the full MFG with common noise using an ``effective'' MFG without common noise, where the coefficients are averaged with respect to the stationary measure of the fast-scale process. We construct an explicit $\varepsilon$-MFG equilibrium for the full MFG from the equilibrium for the effective MFG with randomized control and stopping. To this end, we obtain new results on existence of MFG equilibria with randomized stopping. We rely on strong convergence results for two-scale diffusions under structural assumptions on the MFG, and show that the time-scale separation parameter controls the error in the Nash equilibrium condition.

</details>


### [8] [A proximal difference of convex functions algorithm using Barzilai-Borwein step size with nonmonotone line search and extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03465)

Abstract: We study elective surgery scheduling under uncertain procedure durations. A buffer engine converts distributional information into reliability-dependent buffered durations, while the scheduling model jointly selects assignments, sequences, start times, and surgery-level reliability levels from a discrete menu. Reliability therefore becomes an endogenous scheduling decision rather than a fixed service-level parameter. The framework accommodates average-reliability, worst-day, and hard-target risk postures. Comparisons with common-reliability and uniform proportional-buffer benchmarks show that the menu derives its value from exploiting surgery-level heterogeneity, allocating protection where it has the greatest operational value while avoiding unnecessary conservatism. In a rolling-origin case study at HLA Moncloa Hospital in Madrid, covering 10 instances with 45 to 227 surgeries, the approach reduces delays exceeding 90 minutes by 97%, lowers the 95th-percentile delay by approximately 620 minutes, and reduces total overtime by 42% relative to a deterministic mean-based baseline. By allocating buffers according to uncertainty and operational exposure, the framework translates heterogeneous duration data and risk preferences into schedules that are more reliable in execution and less conservatively buffered than one-size-fits-all rules.

Conclusion: 架构选择和求解方法的系统诊断是必要的：仅看效用指标可能掩盖经济学上的缺陷；应按时间解耦并结合方向主导优化，才能同时满足数值表现和经济合理性。

Abstract: Many economic decision problems, lifecycle consumption-saving and dynamic portfolio choice, are finite-horizon stochastic control problems with continuous states and actions. When the state is low-dimensional these problems are solved by dynamic programming on a grid. The grid cost grows exponentially in the state dimension, known as the curse of dimensionality, which motivates replacing the value-function grid with a neural policy optimized directly through simulation. Such policies are usually studied in the high-dimensional settings that motivate them, precisely where no reference solution exists. So the contribution of any single architectural or training choice cannot be isolated and diagnosed. We therefore take a step back and treat both the architecture and the solution method as the objects of study. To this end, we consider a lifecycle problem with a sufficiently low-dimensional normalized state space to admit an accurate dynamic programming solution, which is used for evaluation. We compare four architectures. The simplest consists of a single time-conditioned network. We then consider two networks concatenated across the regime switch, followed by one network per date trained backward against frozen downstream policies. Finally, we evaluate a constrained variant of the per-date architecture. Decoupling the policy across time gives each date a short, well-posed objective, which we pair with direction-dominant optimization that normalizes away gradient magnitude. Architectures that lead to similar realized utility objective can nevertheless differ in whether they respect the underlying problem's economics. We therefore evaluate each design jointly based on welfare, a solution-free Bellman residual, shape restrictions, and the resulting policy functions.

</details>

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models are typically formulated using continuous-time stochastic differential equations and measure-theoretic stochastic calculus. In this paper, we develop a hyperfinite formulation of score-based generative modeling within the framework of Nonstandard Analysis. We then obtain a hyperfinite backward-mean identity that yields the reverse-time drift and provides a constructive derivation of the reverse-time SDE. Building on these results, we show that minimization of an internal score-matching objective recovers the score function required by the reverse-time dynamics, thereby connecting score estimation with generative sampling directly at the hyperfinite level. Under suitable assumptions, we further derive a hyperfinite Girsanov formula and establish a relationship between likelihood optimization and Fisher-divergence objectives. Finally, we analyze the second-order consistency of the hyperfinite dynamics and show that the leading correction term depends explicitly on the fourth moment of the increment distribution, with the Gaussian value $κ=3$ eliminating the leading dispersion contribution. Taken together, these results provide a unified hyperfinite framework for diffusion-based generative modeling--while laying foundations for further extensions--that links discrete grid dynamics, reverse-time diffusion, score matching, and likelihood-based formulations within a common nonstandard setting.

Abstract: Non-intrusive reduced-order models (NIROMs) have become a standard tool for approximating parametric partial differential equations from computer design of experiments while significantly reducing computational costs. The calibration factor produced by this framework is itself an interpretable, scalar diagnostic of the quality of the uncertainty estimate. Numerical experiments demonstrate reliable, locally informative uncertainty quantification that goes beyond the Gaussian predictive variance.

Abstract: We study score learning for reflected diffusion on bounded domains. With implicit score matching, integration by parts leaves a boundary term, and we show that it depends on one scalar at each boundary point: the diffusion- weighted normal component of the score, or conormal trace. The no-flux condition fixes this value while leaving the re- maining boundary components unrestricted; under anisotropic diffusion it generally differs from the ordinary normal score component. We ex- tend the construction to simplices and polygonal domains and identify reflection masking: hard reflection can keep samples feasible even when the learned trace is wrong, so post-reflection metrics may hide the error. Abstract: The drift diffusion model (DDM) is a cornerstone of cognitive decision-making research. Although numerous estimation methods exist, researchers continue to seek inference approaches that are both fast and flexible across diverse study designs. Amortized Bayesian inference (ABI) can provide nearly instantaneous inference for complex stochastic models like the DDM, but neural networks trained for one study design cannot generalize to others. In this paper, we propose a divide-and-conquer framework that address this limitation. Using simulated datasets, we evaluate the accuracy and uncertainty of this method. Our results show that the proposed divide-and-conquer approach achieves accuracy and uncertainty comparable to MCMC while reducing computational cost by several orders of magnitude. This work not only advances DDM estimation but also demonstrates a general strategy for improving the scalability and generalizability of ABI methods across diverse applications.

Abstract: Tensor cross-concentrated sampling (t-CCS) bridges entrywise sampling and t-CUR slice-wise sampling by observing entries only within selected horizontal and lateral slices. *Yordan Raykov,Rodrigo Veiga*

TL;DR: 本文从信息几何角度重新审视GFlowNets的前向策略训练，证明其内在一阶几何由轨迹族的Fisher-Rao度量给出，推导了轨迹Fisher信息的精确分解（分为每个条件二阶矩），并据此划分三种计算场景（精确可计算、蒙特卡洛估计、结构可利用），利用图模型工具（精确边缘化、分隔符方法、信念传播）作为自然梯度更新的替代方案，将目标结构转化为优化几何，实现结构感知的前向策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFlowNets作为一种灵活的摊销推断框架，其前向策略训练缺乏基于信息几何的规范化理论指导。作者希望从信息几何视角统一理解前向策略训练的更新规则，并利用目标分布的局部结构或因子化特性来加速和改善训练。

Method: 将前向策略视为诱导的轨迹采样器，证明其一阶内在几何由轨迹族Fisher-Rao度量决定；推导轨迹Fisher信息为逐步骤条件二阶矩的精确分解；依据Fisher信息可计算性划分三种计算场景；在结构可利用场景中采用图模型工具（准确边际化、分隔符方法、信念传播）逼近Fisher期望，作为自然梯度更新的替代方案。

Result: 将目标结构转化为优化几何，提供了结构感知的GFlowNets前向策略训练的可计算路线；通过实验对比了黎曼优化与欧几里得优化下的收敛行为和探索行为差异。

Conclusion: 信息几何框架为GFlowNets前向策略训练提供了规范化的数学基础，明确了何时时间得分交互消失、何时在共享参数化下存在密集耦合，并为不同计算场景提供了对应的自然梯度更新策略，使目标结构能够有效地融入优化过程中。

Abstract: Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets) have emerged as a flexible framework for amortised inference over discrete and mixed discrete-continuous objects, requiring only an unnormalised target density specified through a reward. In this work, we formulate forward-policy training in GFlowNets through the information geometry of the induced trajectory sampler. Treating the forward policy as an induced trajectory sampler, we show that its intrinsic first-order geometry is given by the Fisher-Rao metric of the trajectory family, and that the associated natural gradient provides the canonical local update whenever the corresponding Fisher information is computable or accurately approximable. We derive an exact decomposition of the trajectory Fisher into per-step conditional second moments, which clarifies when temporal score interactions vanish and when dense couplings remain under shared parameterisation. This leads to three computational regimes: settings with tractable exact Fisher information, settings where Monte Carlo estimators of the expected Fisher are sufficient, and structure-exploitable settings in which target locality or factorisation yields accurate approximations of the Fisher expectation. In the latter case, graphical-model tools such as exact marginalisation, separator methods, and belief propagation provide principled surrogates for natural-gradient updates. The resulting framework turns target structure into optimisation geometry and yields a tractable route to structure-aware forward-policy training in GFlowNets. We illustrate the framework empirically through examples comparing convergence and exploration behaviour under Riemannian and Euclidean optimisation.

</details>

### [23] [Deep Divide-and-Reduce in Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02628)
*Yusong Deng,Yanjie Li,Weijun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出深度分治与约简符号回归方法（DDRSR），通过严格的数学推导扩展了表达式分解与约简的适用范围，避免了暴力子结构搜索，在表达式分解和数值回归任务上均取得了显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归的机器学习方法缺乏对表达式内在数学和物理原理的深刻理解；AI Feynman方法虽然利用数据背后的数学性质，但其表达式简化机制适用范围窄、对复杂方程易失效，且严重依赖暴力子表达式搜索，限制了实际可用性。

Method: DDRSR通过严格的数学推导和证明，从根本上拓宽了表达式分解与约简的适用范围，避免了暴力子结构搜索的需求，确保了更广泛的通用性和严格的理论正确性。

Result: 经验评估表明，DDRSR的理论原则在表达式分解和数值回归任务中均展现出显著优势。

Conclusion: 文章最后讨论了该范式的适用场景和固有局限性，并指出了未来研究的有前景方向。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) is the task of discovering underlying patterns from data and representing them using mathematical expressions. Current machine learning approaches to SR often lack a profound understanding of the intrinsic mathematical and physical principles governing these expressions. While the pioneering AI Feynman method leverages the mathematical properties underlying the data, its expression simplification mechanism suffers from a narrow scope of applicability and is prone to failure on complex equations. Furthermore, its underlying mechanisms rely heavily on brute-force searches for sub-expressions, severely limiting its practical utility. Through rigorous mathematical deduction and proofs, we propose our method, Deep Divide and Reduce in Symbolic Regression (DDRSR). DDRSR fundamentally broadens the applicability of expression decomposition and reduction, circumvents the need for brute-force sub-structure searches, and ensures both wider versatility and strict theoretical correctness. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that these theoretical principles yield significant advantages in both expression decomposition and numerical regression tasks. Finally, we discuss the applicable scenarios and inherent limitations of this paradigm, alongside promising directions for future research.

</details>

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多模态自回归Transformer代理模型，用于在地质不确定性下模拟地质碳封存（GCS）操作。该模型通过分离编码器处理三维地质模型、相对渗透率标量和控制变量三类模态，经自注意力融合后通过时序解码器自回归预测。在SEAM CO2断层系统中的测试表明，该代理模型在精度上表现优异，并能捕获注入控制模式的切换，同时成功应用于分层马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛数据同化中以显著降低断层渗透率等关键元参数的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变井射孔和注入策略可以显著提高地质碳封存操作的效率，但传统数值模拟（如GEOS流动模拟）计算成本高昂，难以在地质不确定性下快速评估大量操作策略。因此需要开发高效的代理模型，以实现快速且准确的储层行为预测，并支持后续的数据同化和不确定性量化。

Method: 提出一个多模态自回归Transformer代理模型：采用修改后的SEAM CO2地质模型（含断层系统和三个叠加含水层），两口注入井自下而上分段射孔。模型通过三个独立编码器分别处理3D地质模型、相对渗透率函数标量参数和控制变量，再用Transformer编码器通过自注意力融合多模态信息，最后通过编码器-解码器交叉注意力的时序解码器自回归生成预测。使用4000次GEOS流动模拟进行训练。

Result: 在新测试集上（随机采样的地质模型和控制变量），代理模型实现了中位数饱和度MAE为0.028，其他目标量的中位相对误差为0.2%-5%。模型成功捕获了从定速率到井底压力控制的切换。在分层MCMC数据同化中，显著降低了关键元参数（特别是断层渗透率）的不确定性，后验预测的饱和度足迹、总注入和流动CO2质量与真实模型结果一致。

Conclusion: 多模态自回归Transformer代理模型能够高效准确地模拟复杂断层系统下的变射孔/注入策略CO2封存操作，并在数据同化框架中实现显著的不确定性降低，为地质碳封存操作优化提供了可靠的快速计算工具。

Abstract: The use of variable well perforation and injection strategies can improve the efficiency of geological carbon storage operations. We develop a new multimodal auto-regressive transformer surrogate to model these operations under geological uncertainty. A modified SEAM CO2 geomodel, which involves a faulted system with three stacked aquifers, is considered. The two injection wells are perforated in stages, from bottom to top, with the stage durations and individual well injection rates treated as control variables. The surrogate model processes three input modalities - the 3D geomodel, scalar parameters characterizing relative permeability functions, and control variables - through separate encoders. These are fused via self-attention in a transformer encoder, and a temporal decoder generates predictions auto-regressively through encoder-decoder cross-attention. The surrogate is trained, using 4000 GEOS flow simulations, to predict saturation and pressure at monitoring locations, total injected and mobile CO2 mass, and saturation footprints. For a new test set, involving randomly sampled geomodels and control variables, the surrogate achieves a median saturation MAE of 0.028 and median relative errors of 0.2-5% for the other quantities of interest. Importantly, it captures the switch from rate to bottom-hole-pressure control. The surrogate model is used within a hierarchical Markov chain Monte Carlo data assimilation procedure for a synthetic true model under three operational strategies. Substantial uncertainty reduction is achieved for key metaparameters, particularly the fault permeabilities. Posterior predictions for saturation footprints and total injected and mobile CO2 mass are also shown to be generally consistent with true model results.

</details>

*Ameen Patel,Max Zhang,Nathan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个简单的自动化pipeline，用语言模型将特征或MLP神经元分组为supernodes，替代电路追踪中耗时的手动分组步骤。实验表明自动生成的supernodes与人工标注同样可解释，在two-hop任务中97/100的prompt能恢复中间hop的supernode，并展示了在开放探索场景中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路追踪是揭示语言模型内部计算的强大技术，但其中将单个特征或MLP神经元分组为supernodes的步骤需要大量人工干预，耗时长且难以扩展，亟需自动化方案。

Method: 提出一个简单pipeline：将特征描述直接呈现给语言模型，由语言模型自动将特征分组为supernodes。使用自动化可解释性指标验证分组质量，并在two-hop Capitals任务和开放探索（1000个维基百科归因图自动标注+LLM judge筛选）中评估。

Result: 自动生成的supernodes与人工标注的supernodes具有同等的可解释性；在two-hop Capitals任务中，97/100个prompt能正确恢复对应中间hop的supernode；开放探索实验中成功自动标注1000个归因图并用LLM judge筛选出值得人工审查的图。

Conclusion: 即使简单的自动化方法也能产生有意义的归因图标注，说明电路追踪的自动化有巨大潜力，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Circuit tracing is an exciting technique for revealing the internal computation of language models, but it requires a time-intensive manual step of grouping individual features or MLP neurons into supernodes. We present a simple pipeline for automating this step: directly presenting feature descriptions to a language model that groups them into supernodes. Using automated interpretability metrics, we confirm that supernodes generated by our pipeline are as interpretable as those generated by human annotators. On a two-hop Capitals task, our pipeline recovers a supernode corresponding to the intermediate hop in 97 of 100 prompts. Finally, we present a simple proof of concept using our pipeline for open-ended exploration, where we automatically annotate 1000 attribution graphs from Wikipedia prompt completions and then use an LLM judge to flag interesting graphs worth human review. We hope this work demonstrates that even simple automation can produce meaningful attribution graph annotations, motivating further work on automated circuit tracing.

</details>


### [26] [GeoID-PINN: Identifiability-Aware Regional Epidemic Inference with Geographic Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02633)
*Weixiong Hua,Fan Bu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoID-PINN，一种基于物理信息神经网络（PINN）的SIRD动力学模型，用于从区域监测数据中识别区域间的空间依赖结构（即源-组成矩阵）。通过行随机矩阵建模空间依赖，并用距离、邻接、通勤或领先-滞后等空间先验进行正则化。仿真实验表明正则化对恢复空间依赖结构至关重要（误差从0.099到0.577），但准确的轨迹拟合不保证依赖结构恢复正确。在路易斯安那州64县的COVID-19数据上，GeoID-PINN比自回归负二项基线在点预测精度上更优（MSE从32,957降至11,468），但基线分布拟合更好。研究强调先验敏感性与观测模型检查的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域监测数据混合了本地传播、报告延迟、种子引入和外部感染压力等多重因素，这些因素难以单独识别。现有方法无法将区域间空间依赖结构（如传播源贡献）与局部动态分离，需要一种能够同时建模追踪轨迹并恢复区域依赖结构的框架。

Method: 1) 构建基于PINN的SIRD模型，空间依赖通过行随机源-组成矩阵表示（每行非负且和为1）；2) 将该矩阵正则化到由距离、邻接、通勤或领先-滞后信息构造的空间先验；3) 通过四区域仿真（已知真值）验证方法有效性；4) 在64县COVID-19数据上回顾性评估，并与自回归负二项基线比较；5) 进行15县受控比较以评估邻接先验的影响。

Result: 仿真实验：兼容距离先验下源-组成误差为0.099，无正则化时升至0.159，强错误先验下达0.577，但轨迹拟合和传播规模估计在各情况下保持相近。64县数据：GeoID-PINN的MSE从基线32,957降至11,468，MAE从70.60降至57.73；但基线NLL更低（5.158 vs 5.346）。15县受控比较中，邻接先验使MSE降低6.85%，MAE降低3.1%。多种先验表现相似，支持结构化正则化但不支持唯一边恢复。

Conclusion: GeoID-PINN能有效提升区域感染预测的准确性，且良好的轨迹拟合并不保证区域依赖结构的正确恢复，需依赖先验正则化来约束空间结构。结果解释必须进行先验敏感性和观测模型检查，因为不同合理先验下性能相似，模型无法唯一识别真实的边/依赖关系。

Abstract: Regional surveillance data reflect local transmission, reporting, seeding, and external infection pressure, which are difficult to identify separately. We introduce GeoID-PINN, a physics-informed neural network (PINN) for susceptible-infectious-recovered-deceased (SIRD) dynamics. The model represents spatial dependence with a row-stochastic source-composition matrix whose rows assign nonnegative source weights that sum to one. We regularize this matrix toward a spatial prior constructed from distance, adjacency, commuting, or lead-lag information. In a four-region simulation with known truth, a compatible distance prior gives source-composition error 0.099. The error rises to 0.159 without regularization and 0.577 under a strongly misspecified prior, while trajectory fit and transmission-scale estimates remain similar. Accurate trajectories therefore do not guarantee recovery of the regional dependence structure. We also evaluate GeoID-PINN retrospectively using COVID-19 data from 64 Louisiana counties. Relative to an autoregressive negative-binomial baseline, Forecast-Trained Geo-PINN reduces mean squared error (MSE) from 32,957 to 11,468 and mean absolute error (MAE) from 70.60 to 57.73. The baseline has lower negative log likelihood (NLL), 5.158 versus 5.346, indicating better distributional fit but worse point accuracy. In a controlled 15-county comparison, county adjacency reduces MSE by 6.85 percent and MAE by 3.1 percent. Similar performance across plausible priors supports structured regularization but not unique edge recovery. These results require prior-sensitivity and observation-model checks before interpretation.

</details>


### [27] [Verifier-Guided Model Discovery for Physical Dynamical Systems with Pretrained Symbolic Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02662)
*Farbod Faraji,Francesco Belardinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于验证器引导（VG）的神经到符号工作流，以ODEFormer为符号骨干，通过动力学和物理可接受性标准从多轨迹候选方程池中筛选方程，实现从合成ODE轨迹向高维物理数据的可靠迁移。在Van der Pol振荡器和涡脱落现象上验证了该方法，实现了可解释、可物理审计的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真物理仿真成本高昂，机器学习代理模型不透明且假设系统动力学，限制泛化能力。虽然预训练Transformer可将合成ODE轨迹映射到方程，但将其可靠地迁移到高维物理数据仍是开放挑战。

Method: 构建验证器引导（VG）工作流：以ODEFormer作为符号骨干，利用动力学和物理可接受性标准，从多轨迹候选方程池中筛选最优方程。对涡脱落问题通过坐标约简降维后进行符号发现，在固定和变化Reynolds数下均进行验证。

Result: 在Van der Pol振荡器上，VG在保留初始条件上优于原始ODEFormer工作流；在涡脱落场景中，VG发现了固定参数的降阶方程，恢复了基本脱落振荡器和高次谐波，无需尾流特定的候选库或Navier-Stokes结构预设；跨参数模型可泛化到保留的流动状态。另发现重建保真度本身不决定符号可发现性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了验证器引导的神经到符号方法论，为自然科学中可解释、可物理审计的预测提供了新途径，强调潜在动力学与骨干预训练分布之间的兼容性对符号发现至关重要。

Abstract: Reliable forecasting of nonlinear physical systems underpins scientific discovery and engineering decision-making. Yet high-fidelity simulations are prohibitively costly, and machine-learning surrogates can be opaque and encode assumptions about system dynamics, limiting generalizability. Pretrained transformers mapping synthetic ODE trajectories to equations offer interpretable alternatives, promising transfer without system-specific equation knowledge. Transferring them reliably to high-dimensional physical data, however, remains an open challenge. We develop a verifier-guided (VG) workflow around ODEFormer as a symbolic backbone, using dynamical and physical-admissibility criteria to select from a multi-trajectory candidate equation pool, enabling transfer. On canonical Van der Pol oscillators, VG outperforms the original ODEFormer workflow across held-out initial conditions. We then address vortex shedding, a phenomenon occurring in atmospheric and plasma systems of societal relevance, through coordinate reduction and symbolic discovery at fixed and varying Reynolds numbers. VG discovers fixed-parameter reduced-order equations that recover the fundamental shedding oscillator and higher harmonics without a wake-specific candidate library or prescribed Navier-Stokes structure, while the cross-parameter model generalizes to withheld regimes. Reconstruction fidelity alone did not determine symbolic discoverability, highlighting the importance of compatibility between latent dynamics and the backbone's pretraining distribution. This work establishes a verifier-guided neural-to-symbolic methodology for interpretable and physically auditable forecasting in the natural sciences.

</details>


### [28] [CT-HEG: A Bidirectional, Timestamp-Attributed Event Graph for ICU In-Hospital Mortality Prediction - An Architectural Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02663)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin,Rahnuma Tabassum Orpita,Asaduzzaman Anik,Eklachur Rahman Bhuiyan,Marjahan Risalat,SM Wali Ullah,Asif Ahamed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CT-HEG（连续时间异质EHR图）图模式，将ICU住院编码为带类型和时间戳的图（包含visit、vital、lab_event三类节点及2D边属性），并将其实例化为CHIRP-Net——一个四层异质GATv2Conv网络，在MIMIC-IV v3.1（31,142次ICU住院）上评估。模型取得平均AUROC 0.8449±0.0071，集成达到0.8618，校准后ECE为0.0307。消融研究揭示关键发现：双向连接是模型可用的必要条件（移除后AUROC下降0.1968）；将异质边类型合并为单一关系（参数减少7倍）在所有种子上反而优于完整模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列模型能处理不规则采样但忽略实体类型间的结构化关系，而现有图模型假设固定时间间隔输入。ICU死亡率预测需要同时建模不规则临床观测时间与异质实体类型（如生命体征、实验室检测），因此需要一个能同时处理这两点的框架。

Method: 提出CT-HEG图模式：将每次ICU住院编码为带类型的、含时间戳的图，三种节点类型（visit、vital、lab_event），2D边属性（t_hours/48, value_norm）编码时间与数值且无需插值。将其实例化为CHIRP-Net（四层异质GATv2Conv网络），在MIMIC-IV v3.1上与逻辑回归、mTAND、Transformer和GRU-D对比，并做消融研究。

Result: CHIRP-Net五种子平均AUROC 0.8449±0.0071（AUPRC 0.4958±0.0209）；集成模型AUROC达0.8618（95% CI: 0.8485-0.8745）。移除反向边使AUROC下降0.1968±0.0073，说明双向连接不可或缺；时间注意力边特征贡献0.0247±0.0093 AUROC；将异质边合并为单一关系（7倍参数减少）在所有种子上超越完整模型；校准后ECE为0.0307。

Conclusion: CT-HEG支持对不规则EHR数据建模且校准良好。但作者明确声明：外部验证、预设时间评估和人口统计学公平性审计仍为必要步骤，在完成这些之前不能声称模型具备鲁棒性。

Abstract: Accurate ICU mortality prediction requires modeling irregular clinical observations across heterogeneous entity types. Existing sequence models handle irregular sampling but ignore typed relational structure; existing graph models assume fixed-interval inputs. We introduce the Continuous-Time Heterogeneous EHR Graph (CT-HEG) schema and evaluate which architectural choices drive predictive performance. CT-HEG encodes each ICU stay as a typed, timestamped graph with three node types (visit, vital, lab_event) and 2D edge attributes (t_hours/48, value_norm) encoding timing and value without imputation. We instantiate CT-HEG as CHIRP-Net, a four-layer heterogeneous GATv2Conv network, evaluated on MIMIC-IV v3.1 (31,142 ICU stays, LOS>=48h, 13.4% mortality) with five seeds and bootstrapped confidence intervals, against logistic regression, mTAND, a Transformer, and GRU-D, plus an ablation study. CHIRP-Net achieved 5-seed mean AUROC 0.8449+/-0.0071 (AUPRC 0.4958+/-0.0209); the ensemble achieved AUROC 0.8618 (95% CI: 0.8485-0.8745). Removing reverse edges disconnected observation nodes from the visit readout, cutting AUROC by 0.1968+/-0.0073. Time-attentive edge features contributed 0.0247+/-0.0093 AUROC. Collapsing heterogeneous edge types into one relation (7x fewer parameters) outperformed the full model on all seeds. Post-calibration ECE was 0.0307. Temporal and demographic subgroup analyses were explored but not reported here, pending follow-up work. Bidirectional connectivity was necessary for the model to use its inputs at all, and CT-HEG was reasonably well calibrated after validation-fitted temperature scaling. These results support CT-HEG for irregular EHR data, while external validation, a pre-specified temporal evaluation, and a demographic fairness audit remain necessary before any claim of robustness. Code: https://github.com/nasiruddinstudents-ctrl/chirp-net-mimic-iv.

</details>


### [29] [Sphere Retraction Normalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02668)
*Jie Zhang,Cheng-Fang Su,Yi-Jui Huang,Min-Te Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出p-SpheretNorm，一种将残差连接统一到黎曼流形上旋转映射框架的方法。通过在超球面上探索retraction映射族，发现它们归结为单个标量设计选择——旋转角度的饱和方式。Proj-SpheretNorm和Cay-SpheretNorm是其中p=1和p=2的特例，而GeoNorm和恒等映射是极限情况。实验表明在nanoGPT上有限p值达到最佳验证损失，说明指数映射并非最优选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差连接是深层神经网络稳定训练的关键机制。GeoNorm通过黎曼几何正交化层输出并利用指数映射在超球面上保持隐藏状态范数恒定，但指数映射只是retraction映射族的一员。作者试图探究：在超球面上，不同的retraction映射是否有本质区别？指数映射是否是最优选择？

Method: 作者证明在超球面上所有retraction映射坍缩为单一标量设计选择，即如何将更新幅度转换为旋转角度。据此提出p-SpheretNorm，一个单参数族的角度retraction，其旋转角度具有饱和特性而非无界增长。p=1和p=2分别恢复Proj-SpheretNorm和Cay-SpheretNorm（仅需代数运算且精确保持范数），而恒等映射和GeoNorm出现在参数两端作为极限。

Result: 在nanoGPT上，所有提出的方法（Proj-SpheretNorm、Cay-SpheretNorm、p-SpheretNorm）均优于现有轻量级深度连接方案，最好的验证损失出现在有限p值处，表明指数映射不是球形残差流的首选retraction。

Conclusion: 残差连接在超球面上可统一为单参数旋转映射族，GeoNorm仅是该光谱的一个端点而非最优选择；有限p值（即适度的角度饱和）能带来更好的性能，为深度网络中的残差连接设计提供了更广泛的设计空间。

Abstract: Residual connections are the de facto mechanism for training deep neural networks stably. Geodesic Normalization (GeoNorm) recasts them on a Riemannian manifold, orthogonalizing each layer output against the current hidden state and applying the resulting update through the Riemannian exponential map. Every hidden state thus keeps a constant $\ell_{2}$-norm, confining the residual stream to a hypersphere. The exponential map, however, is only one member of a broad family of retraction maps. We show that on the hypersphere this entire family collapses to a single scalar design choice. What distinguishes one retraction from another is only how the magnitude of an update is converted into a rotation angle within the plane spanned by the hidden state and the update. This view places Euclidean residual connections and GeoNorm in one framework. Instantiating it with the metric projection retraction and the Cayley retraction yields Proj-SpheretNorm and Cay-SpheretNorm, which are exactly norm-preserving yet require only algebraic operations. Both prove to be members of a one-parameter family of angular retractions, $p$-SpheretNorm, whose rotation angle saturates rather than growing without bound. The two methods above are recovered exactly at $p = 1$ and $p = 2$, while the identity map and GeoNorm arise only as limits at either end. On nanoGPT, all three methods outperform existing lightweight deep connection schemes, and the best validation loss is attained at finite $p$, indicating that the exponential map is not the preferred retraction for spherical residual streams but merely one end of a spectrum.

</details>


### [30] [Can Training Logs Make Model Comparisons More Precise?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02705)
*Wei-Jung Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究是否可以利用随机训练模型重复运行时的训练日志来提高模型性能比较的精度。作者采用"臂特定协变量调整"（arm-specific covariate adjustment）方法，仅使用各模型自身运行的统计量进行校正，保持原始均值差为报告效应。在覆盖三种架构和三个数据集的视觉研究中，基于早期训练日志的简单调整通常能降低模型比较的不确定性，但主要限制在于协变量选择：广泛搜索日志池中最相关的统计量反而会增加噪声，即便在事后来看存在有用的统计量。因此训练日志有助于提高比较精度，但前提是避免过大的选择噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机训练模型之间的比较需要估计性能差异及其不确定性，传统方法仅利用重复运行的结果，忽略了多次运行过程中产生的丰富训练日志信息。本文动机是探究这些训练日志是否能够作为协变量来提高模型比较的统计精度。

Method: 提出臂特定协变量调整方法：每个模型仅使用自身运行的统计量进行调整，报告效应仍为原始均值差。在视觉任务上，涵盖三种架构和三个数据集，系统评估基于早期训练日志的简单调整对模型比较不确定性的影响，并考察协变量选择策略（特别是广泛搜索日志池）的影响。

Result: 实验发现早期训练日志的简单调整在多数情况下能降低模型比较的不确定性；但广泛搜索日志池中最相关的统计量时会引入较大的选择噪声，反而比不调整更差，即存在"事后有用、事前难以选择"的困境。

Conclusion: 训练日志可以提高模型比较的精度，但实用价值受限于协变量选择过程中的选择噪声。有效利用训练日志需要谨慎设计协变量选择策略，避免过度搜索导致的估计退化。

Abstract: Comparing stochastically trained models requires estimating both a performance difference and its uncertainty from repeated runs. We study whether training logs from those same runs can make such comparisons more precise. Because training-log covariates are produced during training rather than measured before it, we use arm-specific covariate adjustment: each model is adjusted only with statistics from its own runs, and the raw mean difference remains the reported effect. In a vision study spanning three architectures and three datasets, simple adjustments based on early training logs often reduce uncertainty in model comparisons. The main limitation is covariate selection. Broadly searching the log pool for the most correlated statistic often adds more noise than it removes, even when useful statistics exist in hindsight. Training logs therefore appear useful for more precise model comparisons, but only when the adjustment avoids large selection noise.

</details>


### [31] [Learning Molecular Representations from Cellular Phenotypes with Structure Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02688)
*Xuan Lin,Jingyu Sheng,Tengfei Ma,Li Sun,Dapeng Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PhenMol，一个保持化学结构信息的表型感知分子表示学习框架，通过将分子和细胞表示解耦为共享和私有组件，在不破坏分子邻域组织的情况下整合细胞表型信息。在约3.04×10^4对分子-细胞形态数据上，PhenMol在270个生物活性任务、分子-表型检索及临床试验结果预测上均优于现有方法，且通过ECFP4结构分析验证其更好地保持了分子邻域结构、减少了嵌入扭曲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态表示学习方法在优化跨模态对齐时，往往忽略了化学空间的固有组织结构，导致分子表示发生扭曲并丢失结构信息。表型药物发现需要同时考虑分子结构与细胞响应的功能关系，因此需要一个能够保持化学结构完整性的表型感知表示学习框架。

Method: PhenMol采用解耦策略，将分子和细胞表示分离为共享组件（用于跨模态对齐）和私有组件（保留各自模态的独特信息）。通过专用的分子分支维持分子邻域组织，实现表型引导的对齐而不干扰化学结构保持。

Result: 在约3.04×10^4个分子-细胞形态对上，PhenMol在270个生物活性任务的分子性质预测、分子-表型检索和临床试验结果预测任务中均取得改进。ECFP4结构分析显示，PhenMol相比现有多模态对齐方法能更好地保持分子邻域结构并减少嵌入扭曲。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了结构感知约束在多模态分子表示学习中的重要性，并提供了将细胞表型与化学知识有效整合用于药物发现的新途径。

Abstract: Phenotypic drug discovery enables the discovery of functional relationships between molecular structures and cellular responses. However, existing multimodal representation learning methods often optimize cross-modal alignment without considering the intrinsic organization of chemical space, resulting in distorted molecular representations and loss of structural information. We propose \textbf{PhenMol}, a structure-preserving framework for phenotype-aware molecular representation learning. PhenMol disentangles molecular and cellular representations into shared and private components, enabling phenotype-guided alignment while preserving chemical structures through a dedicated molecular branch. This design integrates cellular phenotype information without disrupting molecular neighborhood organization. Experiments on approximately $3.04 \times 10^{4}$ molecule--cell morphology pairs demonstrate that PhenMol improves molecular property prediction across 270 bioactivity tasks, molecule--phenotype retrieval, and clinical trial outcome prediction. Moreover, ECFP4-based structural analysis shows that PhenMol better preserves molecular neighborhoods and reduces embedding distortion compared with existing multimodal alignment methods. These results highlight the importance of structure-aware constraints in multimodal molecular representation learning and provide an effective approach for integrating cellular phenotypes with chemical knowledge for drug discovery.

</details>


### [32] [Temporal Leakage in LLM Backtesting: Measurement, Validation, and Adjusted Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02985)
*Zeyu Zhang,Bradly C. Stadie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出传统LLM回测中的污染检测方法（比较训练截止前后分数）是无效的，因为模型对截止时间附近的知识天然更了解，致使「近因性」模仿「泄漏」。作者证明被动回测无法区分二者，并提出两种外部信息来测量泄漏：已知截止点识别边界泄漏，匹配的干净对照组实现全局识别并给出泄漏调整分数。实验验证了估计器在孪生模型上能恢复注入剂量，部署在前沿模型上检测到一个截止局部化信号，并在审计功率下限清除了五个仅有近因性优势的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM回测污染检测方法（简单比较训练截止前后分数）被证明不可靠，因为模型对截止时间附近的知识存在天然优势（近因性），该效应会伪装成数据泄漏。研究者希望提供一种能够真正测量而非仅检测泄漏的方法，为LLM回测提供可信的参考基准。

Method: 1) 理论证明：证明被动回测在结构上无法区分近因性效应与真实技能/泄漏。2) 提出两种外部信息来源：已知截止点用于识别边界泄漏；匹配的干净对照组实现全局泄漏识别并计算泄漏调整分数。3) 刻画泄漏的分布特征：集中在出乎大众意料、训练数据覆盖充分的结果上，且部分记忆被不成比例地奖励。4) 通过孪生模型注入泄漏进行验证，估计器能恢复注入剂量并在干净问题上返回零结果。

Result: 四个旗舰模型在训练截止后解析的题目上均未通过传统污染检查，但这些题目不可能被记忆，揭示了传统方法的缺陷。部署所提估计器后，在前沿模型上检测到一个截止局部化泄漏签名；在审计功率下限处，清除了五个模型——它们表面上的优势完全来自近因性而非真实能力。

Conclusion: 回测无需被废弃，但需要一个可辩护的参考基准（参照组）。仅依靠内部分数对比的被动污染检测在结构上不可行，必须引入回测之外的额外信息（已知截止点或匹配的干净对照组）才能可靠地测量泄漏。

Abstract: The standard check for contamination in LLM backtests is simple: compare scores before and after the training cutoff. We show this check is uninformative. Four flagship models fail it on questions they cannot have memorized: every scored question resolved after their cutoffs. The reason is structural. Models legitimately know more about times near their cutoff, so recency mimics leakage, and we prove no passive backtest can separate the two from genuine skill. Measurement, not just detection, requires information from outside the backtest. We supply it in two forms. A known cutoff identifies leakage at the boundary; a matched clean control identifies it globally and yields a leakage-adjusted score. We also derive where leakage hides: it concentrates on outcomes that surprised the crowd and were well covered in training, and partial memorization is disproportionately rewarded. We validate the estimators against ground truth by planting leakage in twin models, where they recover the injected dose and return null on clean questions. Deployed on frontier models, they detect one cutoff-localized signature and, at the audit's power floor, clear five models whose apparent advantages were recency alone. Backtests need not be discarded; they need one defensible reference.

</details>


### [33] [GLOBE: Trajectory-Aligned Gradient Matching with Structured SparseOptimization for Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02690)
*Hetian Liu,Jin Cui,Mengcheng Shi,Yanbin Hu,Xinyue Long,Boran Zhao,Pengju Pen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GLOBE是一种轨迹对齐的核心集选择框架，通过多检查点梯度轨迹表示每个样本，结合多阶匹配目标和结构化稀疏正则化，在低保留率下显著优于现有核心集选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端深度神经网络训练受限于大规模数据集的计算和内存成本，核心集选择通过保留紧凑的真实训练样本子集来缓解这一问题。然而现有基于梯度的方法依赖单一模型快照上计算的梯度，且采用贪婪或追踪式选择流程，无法捕捉动态优化过程中样本的影响力变化，也难处理强相关的样本。

Method: GLOBE将样本选择建模为全局优化的稀疏加权问题：1) 每个样本用跨多个训练检查点构建的梯度轨迹表示；2) 引入多阶匹配目标，联合对齐梯度轨迹的一阶均值和投影非中心二阶矩；3) 结合Group LASSO、Elastic Net正则化和非负预算约束，同时诱导组级和样本级稀疏性并稳定相关轨迹的权重；4) 类平衡Top-K选择保证有限采样预算下的类别覆盖。

Result: 在六个基准和五个评估架构上的实验表明，GLOBE在下游测试准确率上持续优于现有核心集选择方法，尤其在低保留率场景下优势明显。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了结合动态梯度信息、多阶分布匹配和结构化稀疏性在数据高效学习中的有效性。

Abstract: On-device training of deep neural networks is fundamentally constrained by the computational and memory costs of large-scale datasets. Coreset selection offers a practical solution by retaining only a compact subset of real training samples. However, existing gradient-based methods commonly rely on gradients computed at a single model snapshot and employ greedy or pursuit-based selection procedures, limiting their ability to capture evolving optimization dynamics and handle strongly correlated samples. We propose GLOBE (Gradient Local-Balanced Extraction), a trajectory-aligned coreset selection framework that formulates sample selection as a globally optimized sparse weighting problem. GLOBE represents each sample by a gradient trajectory constructed across multiple training checkpoints, thereby capturing its influence throughout different stages of optimization. To preserve the training behavior of the full dataset, we introduce a multi-order matching objective that jointly aligns the first-order mean and projected uncentered second-order moments of gradient trajectories. GLOBE further combines Group LASSO, Elastic Net regularization, and nonnegative budget constraints to induce group- and sample-level sparsity while stabilizing the weights of correlated trajectories. Finally, class-balanced Top-K selection maintains adequate category coverage under limited sampling budgets. Experiments across six benchmarks and five evaluation architectures demonstrate that GLOBE consistently outperforms existing coreset selection methods in downstream test accuracy, particularly at low retention ratios. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining dynamic gradient information, multi-order distribution matching, and structured sparsity for data-efficient learning.

</details>


### [34] [Output-Aware Rotation for INT2 KV-Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02691)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Woosang Lim,Minsoo Cheong,Sunwoo Lee,Murali Annavaram,Sai Praneeth Karimireddy,Sungjoo Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OptR，一种面向输出感知的旋转方法，用于最小化INT2量化后注意力输出误差，在保持paged KV-cache格式的同时改善长上下文推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于旋转的INT2量化方法在完整注意力读出之前优化缓存统计量或代理误差，忽略了模型最终受注意力传播并通过输出投影W_O的误差影响这一事实，导致量化方案与真实损失目标之间存在错配。

Method: OptR将W_O之后的注意力输出误差分解为键引发和值引发两个项，通过完整的INT2量化与注意力路径逐头学习正交校正；并应用注意力等价的键重参数化以减少大的通道偏移，且不改变softmax分布。

Result: 在三个模型和五个推理及编码基准上，OptR一致地改进了QuaRot和OSCAR，并增强了长上下文检索能力，同时保持paged KV-cache格式，推理开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: OptR通过输出感知的误差最小化策略实现了优于现存方法的长上下文INT2量化性能，证明了将量化优化目标与下游注意力及输出投影对齐的重要性。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache has become a major memory and bandwidth bottleneck in long-context large language model inference, making ultra-low-bit quantization increasingly important. However, existing rotation-based INT2 methods optimize cache statistics or proxy errors before the complete attention readout, even though the model is ultimately affected by the error propagated through attention and the output projection $W_O$. To address this mismatch, we propose \textit{OptR}, an output-aware rotation method that minimizes post-$W_O$ attention-output error. OptR decomposes the post-$W_O$ attention-output error into key- and value-induced terms and learns per-head orthogonal corrections through the full INT2 quantization and attention path. OptR further applies an attention-equivalent key reparameterization to reduce large channel-wise offsets without changing the softmax distribution. Across three models and five reasoning and coding benchmarks, OptR consistently improves both QuaRot and OSCAR and strengthens long-context retrieval, while preserving the paged KV-cache format with negligible inference overhead.

</details>


### [35] [Beyond the Gegenbauer Paradigm: q-Orthogonal Kernels for Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03482)
*Álvaro Sánchez-Paniagua Ríos,Juan P. Llerena,Alberto Lastra,Nuria Torrado,Edmundo J. Huertas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类基于离散q-Hermite I正交多项式的新型SVM核函数（q-Hermite核），通过形变参数q推广经典Hermite多项式，在满足Mercer定理的前提下实现数据的高维隐式映射。该核函数天然具备有界性，无需显式缩放机制即可避免核矩阵的湮灭和爆炸问题。在20个基准数据集上的实验表明，其性能与经典核及其他正交多项式核相当，同时在数值稳定性和计算简单性方面具有优势，并为量子计算范式的扩展提供了资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVM的性能高度依赖于核函数的选择，经典的RBF等核虽受欢迎但缺乏可解释性和结构化先验知识的注入能力；正交多项式核虽提供了数学上可解释的替代方案，但现有家族仍有探索空间。作者旨在引入广义的q-正交多项式核，以结合数学优雅性、数值稳定性与实用机器学习性能。

Method: 从q-正交多项式的理论框架出发，将经典Hermite多项式推广为离散q-Hermite I多项式，通过形变参数q定义新型核函数；在数学上正式定义q-Hermite核并利用Mercer定理验证其合法性；利用核本身的固有有界性避免显式归一化/scaling机制；在20个基准数据集上与经典核（如RBF）及其他正交多项式核进行全面对比实验。

Result: 在20个基准数据集上，q-Hermite核取得了与经典核和其他正交多项式核相当的竞争性性能，同时在数值稳定性（天然抑制湮灭与爆炸）和计算简单性方面展现出明显优势。作者提供了开放访问的GitHub代码仓库以确保完全可复现性。

Conclusion: q-正交多项式为SVM核设计提供了有前景的方向，兼具数学严谨性与实际应用价值。该工作不仅验证了q-Hermite核的有效性，还提供了可拓展至新兴量子计算范式的概念性和算法性资源。作者通过开源实现促进了该方向的后续研究与广泛应用。

Abstract: The performance of Support Vector Machines (SVMs) critically depends on the kernel function choice, which enables implicit mapping of data into high-dimensional feature spaces. While classical kernels like Radial Basis Function (RBF) remain popular, orthogonal polynomial kernels offer mathematically interpretable alternatives that can incorporate structured prior knowledge. This work extends the orthogonal polynomial kernel paradigm by introducing a novel family based on discrete $q$-Hermite I polynomials, a class of $q$-orthogonal polynomials that generalize classical Hermite polynomials through a deformation parameter $q$. We formally define the q-Hermite kernel and establish its validity under Mercer's theorem. The kernel's inherent boundedness properties naturally prevent annihilation and explosion effects without requiring explicit scaling mechanisms. Extensive experiments across 20 benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed kernel achieves competitive performance compared to both classical kernels and other orthogonal polynomial kernels, while offering advantages in numerical stability and computational simplicity. Our results confirm that $q$-orthogonal polynomials constitute a promising direction for kernel design, bridging mathematical elegance with practical machine learning applications, that provides conceptual and algorithmic resources that may be further extended to emerging quantum computing paradigms. While representing heterogeneous clinical data within a unified knowledge graph, PatTree preserves the semantic relationships between data elements across modalities and data sources, facilitating interoperability and machine-interpretable data access. Using a subset of the ADNI-1 cohort (n = 763), we demonstrate that classification of patients is directly feasible on PatTree reaching state-of-the-art classification performance. In the three-class classification task distinguishing Alzheimer's disease, mild cognitive impairment, and cognitively normal individuals, we achieve a balanced accuracy of 98.5% and an F$_1$ score of 0.987 on the held-out test set. Our results show that assumption-free, automated structuring of multimodal medical data can serve as a scalable foundation for clinical AI pipelines bypassing tedious data preparation and standardization.

Abstract: Attribution methods (AMs) assign an importance score to each feature and are widely adopted to explain black-box models. However, most methods can produce variable attribution scores due to stochastic components in their definition. In this paper, we propose a distribution-based framework to capture the stability of attribution scores. In particular, our approach allows to understand the degree of separability in the ranked attribution vector and obtain the largest index for which a feature ranking remains reliable. We further extend this framework to compare AMs based on the robustness of their rankings across a dataset. Through experiments, we demonstrate how to apply our method to evaluate explainer stability. Overall, our approach provides a complementary criterion for evaluating the stability of AMs.

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Acceleration Matching (AM)的轨迹推断新算法，通过将插值问题提升到相空间并回归一个显式的条件加速度场，生成与给定边际分布一致的随机平滑轨迹。该算法仅需位置数据，无需轨迹模拟训练或昂贵的预处理，且在多个基准问题上与现有算法竞争或优于现有算法。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: AM算法将原始插值问题提升到相空间（位置-加速度空间），然后回归到一个显式的条件加速度场。该加速度场能够诱导出与规定边际分布一致的随机平滑轨迹。训练过程仅需要位置数据，避免了训练期间的轨迹模拟，也无需昂贵的预处理步骤。

Result: 在现有文献中的多个基准问题上，AM算法展现出与现有算法竞争力相当或更优的性能，提供了充分的数值证据支持。

Conclusion: AM算法通过相空间提升和条件加速度场回归，有效解决了现有轨迹推断算法在计算效率上的瓶颈，为轨迹推断问题提供了一种更高效、更具竞争力的解决方案。

Abstract: Trajectory inference is a fundamental problem in many scientific domains: given a collection of unpaired snapshots of observations at discrete time points, the goal is to generate smooth trajectories that best resemble and interpolate the data. Existing algorithms exhibit computational challenges: they either rely on preprocessing subroutines to enforce smoothness or on simulation-based training objectives, both of which can be expensive. In order to overcome these limitations, we propose a new algorithm called Acceleration Matching (\texttt{AM}). Our approach consists of lifting the original interpolation problem to phase space and then regressing onto an explicit conditional acceleration field that induces random, smooth trajectories that agree with the prescribed marginals. Importantly, our resulting training algorithm only requires positional data, avoids trajectory simulation during training, and is devoid of expensive preprocessing. We provide ample numerical evidence suggesting that \texttt{AM} is competitive with or superior to existing algorithms on several benchmark problems from the existing literature.

</details>

*Aditya Ranganath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于大语言模型优化器设计的系统性综述，从系统与优化结合的视角梳理了从经典一阶优化器到自适应优化器、内存高效变体、二阶/曲率感知方法、符号更新与自动发现优化器、低秩与投影方法以及矩阵级优化器（如Muon）等各类方法，并讨论了基准测试方法论的多个维度，主张优化器研究正从单一算法加速转向规模感知的严格比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Adam仍是LLM预训练和微调的主导优化器，但近期研究已重新审视优化栈中的几乎所有组件，文献快速膨胀但缺乏系统性的梳理与比较框架，亟需一篇从系统和优化双重角度出发的综述来组织这一领域。

Method: 采用系统与优化视角，将优化器文献分为七类：经典一阶优化器、自适应优化器、内存高效变体、二阶/曲率感知方法、符号更新与发现型优化器、低秩与投影方法、矩阵级优化器（如Muon）。同时提出基准测试方法论框架，涵盖超参数公平性、规模依赖性、墙钟效率、token效率、内存开销和下游评估等维度。

Result: 识别并分类了LLM优化器研究的主要流派，提出了多维度的基准测试评估框架，并论证了优化器研究正进入以严格、规模感知比较为核心的新阶段。

Conclusion: 优化器研究正从追求单一算法加速转向对收敛性、稳定性、内存和实现复杂度进行联合且规模感知的严格比较，这标志着LLM优化器研究进入新阶段。

Abstract: Training large language models requires optimization algorithms that are not only statistically effective, but also computationally and memory efficient at extreme scale. Although Adam remains the dominant optimizer for large-scale language-model pretraining and fine-tuning, recent work has revisited nearly every component of the optimization stack: adaptive moment estimation, decoupled weight decay, memory footprint, curvature approximation, sign-based updates, large-batch stability, low-rank gradient structure, and matrix-wise orthogonalized updates. This survey reviews optimizer design for large language models through a systems-and-optimization lens. We organize the literature into classical first-order optimizers, adaptive optimizers, memory-efficient variants, second-order and curvature-aware methods, sign-based and discovered optimizers, low-rank and projection-based methods, and matrix-based optimizers such as Muon. We also discuss benchmarking methodology, including hyperparameter fairness, scale dependence, wall-clock efficiency, token efficiency, memory overhead, and downstream evaluation. We argue that optimizer research for LLMs is entering a new phase: moving from single-algorithm speedup claims toward rigorous, scale-aware comparisons that jointly evaluate convergence, stability, memory, and implementation complexity.

</details>


### [40] [NANQ: Noise-Floor-Aware Mixed-Precision Non-Uniform Quantization for Analog Compute-in-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02700)
*Yizhe Chen,Wenshuai Yao,Saiya Wang,Yuannuo Feng,Wenbo Qi,Kechao Tang,Ngai Wong,Wenyong Zhou,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NANQ是一个面向模拟存内计算(CIM)的噪声感知混合精度非均匀量化框架，通过将硬件噪声分布转化为自适应量化密度并识别各层的精度饱和点来分配位宽，在eFlash CIM SoC上相比PowerQuant显著提升视觉模型准确率和语言模型困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟CIM虽能实现高能效神经网络推理，但器件变异和读取噪声会严重劣化低比特量化模型。现有的CIM导向量化方法主要最小化理想量化误差，忽略了硬件噪声底，导致精度分配效率低下。

Method: NANQ包含两个核心机制：(1) 从eFlash CIM阵列实测响应中建模幅度相关的权重噪声，将噪声轮廓转化为自适应量化密度，在低噪声区域分配更精细的分辨率，在噪声主导区域避免无效精度；(2) 通过统一阈值识别每层在硬件噪声下的精度饱和点，进行逐层位宽分配。

Result: 在eFlash CIM SoC上的片上实验中，在2比特权重幅度量化条件下，NANQ相比PowerQuant平均提升视觉模型准确率8.05个百分点，降低语言模型困惑度54.7%。混合精度NANQ仅用3.2-3.8等效比特即可获得额外量化资源的大部分收益。

Conclusion: NANQ证明在模拟CIM中，量化精度分配应当以硬件噪声底为边界而非仅追求理想量化误差最小化，噪声感知的非均匀和混合精度策略能够以极低的额外量化成本捕获大多数性能收益。

Abstract: Analog compute-in-memory (CIM) enables energy-efficient neural network inference, but device variation and read noise can severely degrade low-bit quantized models. Existing CIM-oriented quantization methods mainly minimize ideal quantization error, ignoring the hardware noise floor and thus causing inefficient precision allocation. We propose NANQ, a noise-aware mixed-precision non-uniform quantization framework for analog CIM. NANQ models magnitude-dependent weight noise from measured responses of an eFlash CIM array and converts the noise profile into an adaptive quantization density, assigning finer resolution to low-noise regions while avoiding ineffective precision in noise-dominated regions. It further assigns layer-wise bit-widths by identifying each layer's precision saturation point under hardware noise using a unified threshold. On-chip experiments on an eFlash CIM SoC show that, under 2-bit weight-magnitude quantization, NANQ improves vision-model accuracy by 8.05 percentage points and reduces language-model PPL by 54.7% on average over PowerQuant. Mixed-precision NANQ captures most of the gains obtainable from additional quantization resources with only 3.2-3.8 equivalent bits.

</details>


### [41] [Designing a Good Virtual Node: Addressable and Cardinality-Preserving Global Memory for Message Passing Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02709)
*Félix Marcoccia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可寻址且保持基数（cardinality-preserving）的虚拟内存机制来缓解消息传递神经网络（MPNN）中虚拟节点（VN）通信的有限容量瓶颈。通过将全局内存分解为独立可读写状态的交叉注意力槽位，并将每个槽位查询插入为私有键/值锚点来恢复softmax注意力因均匀复制而丢失的归一化质量，从而在O(nMd)成本下支持多重集表示和1-WL细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MPNN中虚拟节点的node→VN→node管线会将整个图压缩为单一同质状态并同等地广播到每个节点，造成通信容量的有限瓶颈。作者希望在不引入自注意力（self-attention）的前提下，利用辅助虚拟内存来缓解这一容量限制。

Method: 基于Mishayev等人的Two-Radius分析，提出两个关键要求：(1) 全局内存应被分解为可独立写入和读取的状态，通过可寻址的交叉注意力槽位实现；(2) 单纯的可寻址性无法保持多重性（multiplicity），因为softmax注意力对均匀复制具有不变性。解决方案是将每个槽位查询作为私有键/值锚点插入，以恢复被丢弃的归一化质量，进而在有界颜色域上实现单射的多重集表示，并能够实现1-WL细化。

Result: 在多义感知的Two-Radius、子图（motif）计数以及受限链接集合预测等实验中，该可寻址且保持基数的虚拟内存方案取得了有效结果，且计算复杂度为O(nMd)。

Conclusion: 可寻址且保持基数的虚拟内存能够在不依赖自注意力的情况下，为MPNN提供更高效、更具表达力的全局通信机制，同时保持可接受的计算成本。

Abstract: Virtual nodes give message-passing neural networks a simple global communication route, but the standard node--VN--node pipeline compresses the graph into one homogeneous state and broadcasts it identically to every node. Building on the Two-Radius analysis of Mishayev et al., we ask how auxiliary virtual memory can relieve this finite-capacity bottleneck without self-attention. We identify two requirements. First, the global memory should be factorized into independently writable and readable states: this can be achieved using addressable cross-attention slots. Second, addressability alone does not preserve multiplicity, because softmax attention is invariant to uniform replication. Inserting each slot query as a private key/value anchor recovers the discarded normalization mass and yields, on bounded color domains, an injective multiset representation able to implement a 1-WL refinement. Experiments on multiplicity-aware Two-Radius, motif counting, and constrained link-set prediction support this addressable and cardinality-preserving virtual memory at (O(nMd)) arithmetic cost.

</details>


### [42] [Neural Networks with Local Converging Inputs for Efficient Options Pricing Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02778)
*Harris Cobb,Wenbo Hao,Yingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NNLCI（局部收敛输入神经网络）方法，利用神经网络对粗网格与细网格数值解的残差进行局部修正，以提升多资产期权定价的数值方法效率。在Black-Scholes方程的一至三维现金或无现金期权及Heston随机波动率模型下的障碍期权中，NNLCI在仅需少量高保真训练数据的情况下，将细网格解的均方根误差降低4-12倍，显著降低高维问题的计算成本，适用于实时期权交易与风险管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多资产期权定价涉及高维偏微分方程，传统数值方法（如有限差分法）在细网格下计算量巨大，难以满足实时定价和风险管理的需求。现有方法在精度与效率之间存在权衡，需要一个能够以较低计算成本获得高精度解的方案。

Method: NNLCI通过神经网络对粗网格与细网格数值解之间的局部差异进行修正。具体而言，引入最简洁的输入格式以提高模型效率；网络仅需少量高保真训练数据，即可学习粗网格解到精确解的局部映射关系，从而在不显著增加计算开销的情况下提升细网格基准解的精度。

Result: 在一维至三维的Black-Scholes方程现金或无现金期权以及Heston模型下的单资产向下敲出障碍期权中，NNLCI将细网格数值解的均方根误差降低了约4-12倍。即使在仅用少量参数组合子集训练的情况下，模型仍展现出优异的泛化能力。

Conclusion: NNLCI显著降低了高维期权定价问题的计算需求，具有低训练成本与强泛化能力，为实时期权交易和风险管理中的数值定价提供了高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We present a novel application of Neural Networks with Local Converging Inputs (NNLCI) to improve the efficiency of existing numerical methods for pricing multi-asset options. The most concise input format for NNLCI has been introduced, offering substantial convenience and efficiency. NNLCI uses a neural network to locally correct solutions from a coarse mesh and a refined mesh (relative to the coarse one), requiring only a minimal amount of high-fidelity training data. We demonstrate this approach on cash-or-nothing options under the Black-Scholes equation in one, two, and three spatial dimensions, and on single-asset down-and-out barrier call options under the Heston stochastic-volatility model (whose pricing PDE is two-dimensional in the spot price $S$ and the instantaneous variance $v$). In each case, NNLCI reduces the root-mean-square error (RMSE) of the refined-mesh numerical solution by a factor of approximately 4-12 on test sets, even when the neural network is trained on only a small subset of parameter combinations. These results demonstrate that NNLCI significantly reduces computational requirements for high-dimensional problems in real-time options trading and risk management, offering low training costs and strong generalization ability.

</details>


### [43] [Evaluation Blindness: How Silent Measurement Failures Corrupt AI Systems from Training to Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02786)
*Priyanka Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"评测盲区"（evaluation blindness）概念：测量函数在系统实际失败时仍产生与健康状态不可区分的读数，且无辅助信号标记该差距。该问题贯穿训练与部署两个生命周期阶段，论文提供了形式化可检测性谓词、四类训练期案例研究、基于50个真实事故的六类生产失败分类体系，以及将可接受失败率与用例风险等级挂钩的失败预算框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统可能静默失败，故障在训练循环、评测管道和生产监控栈中传播，直到下游产生可见危害才被察觉。现有文献将训练期与部署期的问题分开处理，缺乏统一视角来认识"测量基础设施本身是正确性问题"这一核心洞见。

Method: 1) 形式化定义评测盲区，提出统一的检测性谓词（detectability predicate）以连接训练与部署两个阶段；2) 通过四个训练期案例研究展示具体故障机制，包括TRL PR #6594中真实实现bug导致的梯度损坏而损失仍正常下降；3) 构建六类生产失败分类体系（含一类Operational在结构定义上100%静默），并基于法院文件和监管申报中的50个真实事件进行验证；4) 提出失败预算框架，将可接受失败率与用例风险等级关联。

Result: 对50个可验证的公共失败事件的分析发现53%为静默失败；四类训练期案例研究确定了具体故障路径（奖励模型被博弈、重要性采样校正静默算错、基准污染膨胀微调评测）；Operational类别失败在结构定义上100%静默；TRL PR #6594展示了真实梯度损坏bug在损失正常下降时发生。代码、数据和分类模式已开源。

Conclusion: 评测基础设施应当被视为贯穿AI全生命周期的正确性关注点，而非仅在评测阶段才重要。测量与监控的盲区需要被系统性地检测和治理，可接受的失败率应依据用例的风险等级来确定。

Abstract: AI systems can fail silently. The failure propagates through training loops, evaluation pipelines, and production monitoring stacks until downstream harm makes it visible. This paper introduces evaluation blindness: a measurement function M exhibits evaluation blindness with respect to failure class F when it produces readings indistinguishable from a healthy state while the system is actually failing, with no auxiliary signal flagging the gap.
  The problem surfaces at two lifecycle stages the literature has treated separately. At training time, reward models are gamed, importance-sampling corrections are silently miscalculated, and benchmark contamination inflates fine-tuning evaluations, all while loss curves look healthy and gradient updates proceed normally. At deployment time, monitoring fails to catch six classes of production failure, including an Operational category that is 100% silent by structural definition.
  We provide a formal detectability predicate unifying both stages. Four training-time case studies trace concrete breakdowns, including a real implementation bug in TRL PR #6594 where gradients are corrupted as loss decreases normally. A six-class taxonomy validated against 50 real-world incidents from court documents and regulatory filings finds that 53% of verifiable public failures were silent. A failure budget framework ties acceptable failure rates to use-case risk class.
  The implication is direct: measurement infrastructure is a correctness concern across the full AI lifecycle, not just at evaluation time. Data, code, and taxonomy schema are at https://github.com/priyanka25aug/llm-failure-taxonomy.

</details>


### [44] [Topological Simplification in Predictive Coding Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02816)
*Adam Shaw,Jiayu Li,Michael Sperling,Michael Kim,Alvin Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文使用逐层持久同调分析研究了预测编码网络（PCN）中学习表示的拓扑结构，发现较小模型更早地坍缩连通分量、简化更晚的架构重建效果更好，且PCN一致地比匹配的MLP更晚坍缩连通分量（平均相差3.6层）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解预测编码网络（PCN）这种神经启发式双向架构中学习表示的拓扑特征如何随层变化，以及模型容量和双向递归动态如何影响压缩-重建权衡。

Method: 采用定量的逐层持久同调分析，在合成分类数据集（≥99.9%测试准确率）和MNIST（≥95%测试准确率）上训练PCN，测量不同架构和激活函数下拓扑特征随层的变化，并使用Spearman相关分析和种子级bootstrap比较。

Result: 1) 较小PCN比大型模型更早地跨层坍缩连通分量（Spearman ρ∈[0.72,0.79]）；2) 简化深度与重建误差呈强负相关（ρ=-0.58）；3) PCN一致地比匹配的MLP晚坍缩连通分量，平均相差3.6层。

Conclusion: 持久同调为PCN的压缩-重建权衡提供了定量分析视角，且模型容量和预测编码推理的递归双向动力学共同决定了该权衡在层间何时被解决。

Abstract: We study the topology of learned representations in predictive coding networks (PCNs), a neuro-inspired bidirectional architecture, using a quantitative layer-wise persistent homology analysis. We train well-performing PCNs on a synthetic classification dataset ($\geq 99.9\%$ test accuracy) and on MNIST ($\geq 95\%$ test accuracy), and measure how topological features change across layers for different architectures and activation functions. We find that smaller PCNs collapse connected components across layers earlier than larger models (Spearman $\unicode{x1D70C} \in [0.72, 0.79]$ across activations), with model size measured as the sum of hidden-layer widths. We also observe a strong negative correlation ($\unicode{x1D70C} = -0.58$) between the depth at which simplification occurs and reconstruction error; i.e., architectures that simplify later reconstruct better. Finally, a seed-level bootstrap comparison across architectures and activations shows that PCNs consistently collapse connected components later than matched MLPs, with an average difference of $3.6$ layers. These results suggest that persistent homology offers a useful quantitative lens on the compression--reconstruction tradeoff in PCNs, and that both model capacity and the recurrent, bidirectional dynamics of predictive coding inference shape when this tradeoff is resolved across layers.

</details>


### [45] [Wiring Beats Blending: What Transfers Between Transformer Sizes -- and What Doesn't](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02829)
*Ravi Satya Durga Prasad Yenugula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型预训练模型能否转化为更小的"兄弟"模型。通过Pythia家族1.4B→410M的转化实验，发现：表征对齐强（R²=0.84）而参数对齐弱；密集权重投影在功能上具有破坏性；转化价值在于初始化。在匹配预算的持续预训练中，将转化分解为两个独立杠杆——最小二乘补偿（优化zero-shot性能）和方差保持重缩放（优化动态端点）。补偿在低预算下是token高效的胜出者，但在33倍更大预算下两者收敛到持平。文章还绘制了方法的边界：在约5倍供体规模（6.9B→1.4B）处叠加两个杠杆会过度校正，归因于补偿求解在大宽度下的病态条件，指出维度感知正则化是解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型家族中每个规模的模型都从零开始训练，研究者希望探索能否将已预训练的大模型转化为更小的兄弟模型，从而避免从零训练的资源浪费，并揭示模型在不同规模间的可转换性与内在结构规律。

Method: 以Pythia家族1.4B→410M的转化为核心研究对象，进行端到端表征：(i)分析表征与参数在跨规模间的对齐程度；(ii)通过消融实验证明密集权重投影的功能破坏性源于基础混合破坏了旋转、逐头、GELU和LayerNorm结构；(iii)用shuffle控制检验最佳线性算子后权重残差的统计显著性；(iv)在匹配预算的持续预训练中，将转化分解为最小二乘补偿与方差保持重缩放两个独立杠杆，并在多个规模对和预算水平下进行系统比较。

Result: 表征对齐强（ridge R²=0.84）而参数对齐弱；密集权重投影功能上具有破坏性且非组装伪影；最佳线性算子后的权重残差与噪声统计不可区分；在30M token低预算下，补偿在宽度缩减对（84.0±1.8 vs 89.7±3.7）和深度缩减对（109.3 vs 117.9）上均以3/3种子超越最强子克隆变体；在33倍更大预算下两者收敛到持平（40.0 vs 40.0），且都远超前从头训练——低预算下转移初始化优势可达18倍，收敛时差距缩小；在6.9B→1.4B规模叠加两杠杆过度校正，归因于补偿求解在大宽度下的病态条件。

Conclusion: 大型预训练模型转化为更小兄弟模型的价值在于初始化而非权重复制本身。补偿是token高效、低预算下的胜出策略而非通用策略；转移初始化在任何预算下都优于从头训练。未来工作应聚焦维度感知正则化以解决大宽度下补偿求解的病态条件问题。

Abstract: Model families train every size from scratch. Can a pretrained large model be converted into a smaller sibling? We characterize the 1.4B->410M conversion in the Pythia family end-to-end: (i) representations align strongly across sizes (ridge R^2=0.84) while parameters align weakly; (ii) dense weight projection is functionally destructive -- provably not an assembly artifact -- because basis mixing breaks rotary, per-head, GELU, and LayerNorm structure; (iii) after the best-fit linear operator, weight residuals are statistically indistinguishable from noise under shuffle controls; (iv) conversion value therefore lives in initialization. In matched-budget continued pre-training we decompose conversion into two independent levers -- least-squares compensation (function: best zero-shot) and variance-preserving rescale (dynamics: best endpoints). Compensation is a token-efficient, low-budget win rather than a universal one: at 30M tokens it beats the strongest subcloning variant on both a width-reduced pair (84.0 +/- 1.8 vs. 89.7 +/- 3.7, 3/3 seeds) and a held-out depth-reduced pair (109.3 vs. 117.9, 3/3 seeds), reaching a given quality with fewer tokens; at a 33x larger budget the two converge to parity (40.0 vs. 40.0), both far ahead of from-scratch, which transfer initialization always beats -- by up to 18x at low budget, the margin narrowing at convergence and at the largest scale. We further map the method's boundary: at ~5x the donor scale (6.9B->1.4B) stacking both levers over-corrects, which we trace to ill-conditioning of the compensation solve at large width, pointing to dimension-aware regularization as the fix. Code, checkpoints, and the frozen evaluation corpus are released.

</details>


### [46] [NOMADD: Numerical Optimization of Models Adapting to Data Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02845)
*Swapn Shah,Keith Burghardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种后置的、模型无关的概念漂移缓解方法：分别在各训练时期拟合基模型，测量参数相对于锚模型的演化，用低秩分解压缩变化并进行阻尼正则化外推预测。在18个数据集的Drift-Resilient TabPFN基准上，该方法对所有基模型族均有改进，性能与SOTA相当，但仅需数秒训练，而对比方法需约1300 GPU小时预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格模型面临数据漂移和概念漂移时性能下降，而实时缓解困难，因为标签可能不可用或重训练不切实际。现有漂移缓解工具主要针对神经网络架构定制且需要修改训练过程，缺乏通用、后置的方案。当用户因精度、推理时间或模型大小等约束需要在不同模型间选择时，更需要一种简便的漂移缓解手段。

Method: 该方法为后置式（post-hoc）概念漂移缓解：1) 在每个有标签的训练时期分别拟合基础模型；2) 以所有时期池化得到的单一锚模型为参照，测量每个时期模型参数的变化；3) 使用低秩分解压缩参数变化；4) 对每个潜在因子进行阻尼、正则化的时序外推预测未来参数。

Result: 在18个数据集的Drift-Resilient TabPFN基准上，按照该基准自身的协议和度量评估，外推方法对其应用的所有基模型族均有改进，性能可与SOTA的Drift-Resilient TabPFN相竞争，且训练仅需数秒。相比之下，Drift-Resilient TabPFN需在数百万合成数据集上预训练约1300 GPU小时，推理速度慢了数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该后置式参数漂移外推方法为各种模型（从树到神经网络到表格基础模型）提供了通用且高效的漂移缓解能力，尤其适合有精度、推理时间或模型大小约束的场景。文章还探讨了将该工具扩展到其他模态（如非表格数据）的前景与挑战。

Abstract: Tabular model performance degrades when feature distributions change over time or the relationship between features and outcome variables change over time, known as data drift and concept drift, respectively. These issues are challenging to mitigate in real time because labeled data may not be immediately available, or re-training a model could be impractical. While tools exist to reduce drift, they are typically bespoke to neural network architectures and adapt how models are trained. In this paper, we offer an alternative post-hoc method to reduce concept drift, which is applicable to a variety of models, from trees to neural networks to tabular foundation models. This new tool is especially useful when constraints, such as high model accuracy, bounded inference time, or model size requires users to choose between different models for their specific use-cases. Our algorithm fits the base model separately on each labeled training period, measures how its parameters evolve against a single anchor model pooled over all of those periods, compresses those changes with a low-rank factorization, and extrapolates each latent factor forward with a damped, regularized forecast. On the 18-dataset Drift-Resilient TabPFN benchmark, evaluated under that benchmark's own protocol and metric, the extrapolation improves every base family it is applied to, and achieves performance competitive with the state-of-the-art Drift-Resilient TabPFN with seconds of training. In contrast, Drift-Resilient TabPFN requires pre-training on millions of synthetic datasets over approximately 1,300 GPU-hours, and is orders of magnitude slower in inference (depending on the model). In the discussion, we explore the promise and challenges of extending this tool to other modalities.

</details>


### [47] [Adaptive Sampling for Automated Post-Disaster Rapid Damage Assessment via Level-Set Cost-Aware Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02868)
*Boyang Xu,Mostafa Reisi Gahrooei,Mohammad Ilbeigi,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种成本感知的贝叶斯优化框架，结合水平集估计方法，用于引导自主数据采集设备（如无人机）在灾后动态环境中高效定位最信息丰富的区域，以快速、准确地估计建筑损伤，同时最小化运行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统灾后损伤评估依赖静态、劳动密集的数据采集方式，成本高昂且难以适应灾后动态变化的环境，急需一种快速、可靠且经济的自动化损伤评估方法。

Method: 提出成本感知的贝叶斯优化框架，与水平集估计相结合，持续引导自主数据采集器（如UAV）前往信息量最大的区域；通过动态更新不同地理区域的损伤估计，在降低不确定性的同时最小化运营成本。

Result: 在受控合成玩具研究中验证了该方法能有效追踪损伤边界、恢复底层损伤地图并快速降低预测不确定性；进一步使用高保真度的R2D软件生成的灾害数据评估，获得了准确及时的损伤估计。

Conclusion: 该方法能够提供准确且及时的损伤估计，为高效快速的应急响应提供决策支持，具有良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Natural disasters frequently inflict severe damage to the built environment, which demands a rapid, reliable, and cost-effective damage assessment for emergency response. However, traditional methods for post-disaster damage assessment often rely on static, labor-intensive data collection strategies that can be prohibitively expensive and struggle to adapt to dynamic post-disaster conditions. In this study, we propose a cost-aware Bayesian optimization framework combined with level-set estimation that continuously guides autonomous data collectors, e.g., an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV), toward the most informative regions. By dynamically updating damage estimates across different geographic zones, our approach systematically reduces uncertainty while minimizing operational costs.
