For anonymous review, our code is available at the following the link: https://github.com/gitrxh/ADRS-arxiv

</details>


### [82] [SAKI: Score-Aware Low-Rank Key Indexing for Long-Context KV Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03228)
*Lin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAKI，一种无需训练的KV缓存索引方法，直接通过协方差加权的低秩目标来保留注意力分数而非键重建质量，在多个LLM上优于PCA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的低秩KV缓存方法要么保留模型权重、要么保留键方差，但这两者都不能直接反映推理过程中实际使用的注意力分数，导致压缩目标与真实推理目标不一致。

Method: 推导出秩r键压缩引起的期望注意力分数失真，证明其对应一个协方差加权的低秩目标。在边际条件下，控制该失真也能改善top-k召回。最优秩r解具有闭式非对称分解形式，通过对协方差加权的查询-键算子进行SVD获得，由此引出SAKI方法。

Result: 在LLaMA 3.1 8B、Qwen 2.5 7B、Mistral 7B v0.1和Llama 3.2 3B上，SAKI在所有测试秩下均优于key PCA。在秩32时，消除了PCA剩余top-64召回误差的13-30%，LLaMA 3.1 8B从0.748提升到0.799，Qwen 2.5 7B从0.786提升到0.850。每模型68-89%的注意力头得到改善，深层收益最大。预测分数MSE与实际测量的Pearson相关系数为0.997，消融实验确认增益来自注意力分数目标的优化。

Conclusion: 直接优化注意力分数目标而非键重建质量是KV缓存压缩的关键；对评分算子的分析还解释了为何仅权重、不变子空间和键重建方法可能次优。

Abstract: Existing low rank KV cache methods preserve either model weights or key variance, neither of which directly reflects the attention scores used during inference. We derive the expected attention score distortion caused by rank r key compression and show that it yields a covariance weighted low rank objective. Under a margin condition, controlling this distortion also improves top k recall. The optimal rank r solution has a closed form asymmetric factorization obtained from the SVD of the covariance weighted query key operator. This motivates SAKI, a training free KV cache index that directly preserves attention scores rather than key reconstruction quality.
  Across LLaMA 3.1 8B, Qwen 2.5 7B, Mistral 7B v0.1, and Llama 3.2 3B, SAKI outperforms key PCA at every tested rank. At rank 32, it removes 13 to 30 percent of PCA's remaining top 64 recall error, including improvements from 0.748 to 0.799 on LLaMA 3.1 8B and from 0.786 to 0.850 on Qwen 2.5 7B. It improves 68 to 89 percent of attention heads per model, with the largest gains in deeper layers. Predicted score MSE reductions closely match empirical measurements, with a Pearson correlation of 0.997, while ablation studies confirm that the gains arise from optimizing the attention score objective rather than covariance weighting alone. Analysis of the scoring operator further explains why weight only, invariant subspace, and key reconstruction methods can be suboptimal.

</details>


### [83] [FinVerse: Financial Time-Series Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03259)
*Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Seunghan Lee,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FinVerse，一个面向金融领域的时间序列预测基准，包含116,897个金融时间序列（1.711亿个观测值），并基于各序列的经济含义为其分配最合适的评估指标（11个指标族、78个评估指标）。通过分析43个公开时间序列基础模型的预测表现，发现通用预测标准下的强性能不一定能转化为有用的金融预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列预测基准通常使用统一的基于误差的指标来评估异构序列，但强预测误差表现并不等同于模型能支持实际领域的决策需求。例如在股票预测中，正确预测涨跌方向比最小化逐点误差更能直接影响实际收益。因此需要一种更贴近真实决策场景的领域感知评估方式。

Method: 构建了FinVerse金融时间序列预测基准，包含116,897个金融时间序列（1.711亿观测值），其中60,232个序列（1,740万观测值）因其经济相关性被选为评估目标。与通用基准不同，FinVerse定义了11个指标族共78个评估指标，并根据每条时间序列的经济含义为其分配最合适的评估指标。

Result: 对43个公开时间序列预测基础模型的分析表明，模型在通用预测标准下表现良好，但并不能保证其在金融预测中产生有用的结果，凸显了领域感知基准对评估模型真实决策支持能力的重要性。

Conclusion: FinVerse强调了需要领域感知的时间序列预测基准，以在更接近真实世界决策的目标下评估模型性能，弥补通用基准与实际应用场景之间的差距。

Abstract: As time-series foundation models have emerged, the need for benchmarks that can evaluate their forecasting ability in meaningful ways has become increasingly important. Existing time-series forecasting benchmarks provide useful standardized comparisons, but they often evaluate heterogeneous series with uniform error-based metrics. Strong performance under such metrics does not necessarily imply that a model's forecasts will support the best real-world decisions across domains. For example, in stock forecasting, correctly predicting whether a price will rise or fall can be more directly relevant to realized returns than minimizing point-wise forecast error alone. To this end, we introduce FinVerse, a finance-domain time-series forecasting benchmark that takes a first step toward more realistic evaluation. The released FinVerse data artifact contains 116,897 financial time series with 171.1M observations, of which 60,232 series with 17.4M observations are selected as evaluated targets based on their economic relevance to financial decisions. Unlike generic forecasting benchmarks that primarily emphasize uniform point-forecast or probabilistic accuracy, FinVerse defines 11 metric families comprising 78 evaluation metrics and assigns the most appropriate evaluation metrics to each individual time series based on its underlying economic meaning. Our analysis of 43 public time-series forecasting foundation models shows that strong performance under generic forecasting criteria does not necessarily translate into useful financial forecasts. This finding highlights the need for domain-aware benchmarks that evaluate models under objectives closer to real-world decision making.

</details>


### [84] [ED-DiT: Physics-Guided Diffusion Pretraining for Transferable Molecular Representations from Electron Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03260)
*Liang Shuang,Haocheng Wang,Jiayi Song,Shuquan Ye,Ben Fei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ED-DiT，一种基于物理引导的扩散Transformer，用于在电子密度点云上进行自监督预训练。通过重建被破坏和部分掩蔽的对数密度场，并引入电子数一致性约束，预训练编码器可有效迁移到属性预测、开/闭壳层分类、分子-电子密度检索和分子条件电子密度预测等多种任务。在EDBench六个任务上，ED-DiT一致优于从零训练的同类架构，尤其在标签稀少时优势显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练在迁移学习中展现了强大潜力，但在基于电子密度的分子学习领域尚未被充分探索。电子密度提供了分子电子结构的连续三维描述，能够同时捕捉局部空间模式和全局物理量。作者希望探索电子密度场能否用于自监督预训练，以学习可跨多种电子结构相关任务迁移的共享表示。

Method: ED-DiT是物理引导的扩散Transformer，在电子密度点云上执行自监督预训练。其核心思想是通过重建跨扩散噪声水平的被破坏和部分掩蔽的对数密度场来学习可复用表示，同时引入电子数一致性约束以保持总电子质量。预训练得到的编码器可适配到多种下游任务，包括属性预测、开闭合壳层分类、分子-电子密度检索和分子条件电子密度预测。

Result: 在EDBench的六个任务上，ED-DiT一致优于同架构从零训练的结果，尤其在有限监督条件下优势更为突出。在分子条件电子密度预测任务中，RMSE从2.2474降至1.3753并超越现有多考基线；在仅使用10%标签时，轨道能量预测RMSE从0.0293改善至0.0138。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了物理引导的电子密度预训练在学习可迁移分子表示方面的有效性，表明电子密度场作为自监督预训练信号具有显著潜力。

Abstract: Pretraining has shown strong potential for learning transferable representations, yet it remains underexplored for electron-density-based molecular learning. Electron density provides a continuous three-dimensional description of molecular electronic structure, capturing both local spatial patterns and global physical quantities. This raises a key question: can electron-density fields be used for self-supervised pretraining to learn a shared representation that transfers across diverse electronic-structure-related tasks? We propose ED-DiT, a physics-guided Diffusion Transformer for self-supervised pretraining on electron-density point clouds. ED-DiT learns reusable representations by reconstructing corrupted and partially masked log-density fields across diffusion noise levels. An electron-number consistency constraint is further introduced to preserve the total electronic mass. The pretrained encoder can be adapted to property prediction, open-/closed-shell classification, molecule-electron-density retrieval, and molecule-conditioned electron-density prediction. Experiments on six EDBench tasks show that ED-DiT consistently outperforms the same architecture trained from scratch, especially under limited supervision. For molecule-conditioned electron-density prediction, it reduces RMSE from 2.2474 to 1.3753 and surpasses the available baseline. With only 10% labels, it improves orbital energy prediction RMSE from 0.0293 to 0.0138. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of physics-guided electron-density pretraining for learning transferable molecular representations.

</details>


### [85] [The Ignition Is Real, and It Lives at the Readout: Latent composition, difficulty-clocked ignition, and the interface-constituted commit in a recurrent-depth reasoner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03263)
*Simon Lam-Muir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究检验了"组合点火"(compositional ignition)现象的真实性——它是一个真实的计算过程、仪器假象，还是从语言训练数据中继承的产物。研究者从零开始独立复现了一个已发表的30M参数循环深度推理模型，通过预先注册的验证门控、双通道（词汇读出与隐藏状态）分辨率测量等手段确认：点火是真实的且存在于读出层，但早期提出的"速度低谷"主张因坐标依赖性而被撤回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究中报道的"组合点火"现象（即模型在推理过程中突然切换到组合式计算模式的时刻）存在三种可能的解释：真实计算、仪器/测量假象、或从训练数据中继承的先验。需要通过受控实验区分这些假设，并严格验证该现象的因果性质与测量位置。

Method: 采用预先注册的严格实验协议：(1) 从零开始独立复现已发表的30M参数循环深度推理器（相同配方和种子）；(2) 使用全签名门控验证复现保真度；(3) 同时测量词汇读出层和隐藏状态两个通道的分辨率；(4) 对比两个同种子但训练轨迹不同的实现；(5) 预先设定验证准则并冻结所有判断标准；通过条件化决策间隔分析、隐藏状态几何分析、径向/角向位移分解等手段量化点火特征。

Result: 点火是真实的且位于读出层：到达时间随问题深度规律上升，分辨率锐利且稳定。在决策承诺时，决策间隔跳跃5.8-8.0 logits，96%情况下超过近阈值非事件步的90分位数。隐藏状态方向在原始几何中发生突变，符合预注册准则；随后在两个通道中冻结（角向步长从52.9°降至1.2°），随后的位移主要为径向（平方范数占比0.961）且对读出层不可见（径向logit效应≤5.7e-6）。然而，解码器LayerNorm坐标下的复合主张未获确认；早期"速度低谷"主张因依赖坐标而被撤回；中间状态无法通过绑定读出恢复（relay 0.00）。

Conclusion: 组合点火在循环深度推理模型中是真实的计算涌现现象，且其具体位置在词汇读出层而非隐藏状态本身。但研究同时揭示了严格科学验证的价值：部分先前主张（如速度低谷）经规范化控制后不成立，且解码器坐标下的部分复合主张未达到预注册标准。研究者以完全透明的方式（预注册准则、预测账本、包括撤回自身主张）执行了验证，展现了科学严谨性。

Abstract: We test whether the "compositional ignition" reported in latent-reasoning models is real computation, an instrument artifact, or inherited from verbal training data. We grow an independent realization of a published 30M-parameter recurrent-depth reasoner from scratch (same recipe and seed), film its development, certify fidelity through a pre-registered whole-signature gate, and measure resolution in two channels at once: the vocabulary readout and the hidden state. The ignition is real and lives at the readout: arrival time rises lawfully with problem depth, resolution is sharp and holds, and the signature reproduces across two same-seed realizations with divergent training trajectories. At commitment the decision margin jumps 5.8-8.0 logits in one iteration, exceeding the 90th percentile of near-threshold non-event steps in 96% of cases; the signed margin's zero-crossing there is definitional and carries no evidential weight, so the evidence is that conditioned magnitude. The hidden-state direction snaps in raw geometry, meeting its pre-registered criterion (in the decoder's LayerNorm coordinates it attenuates just below our bar, so the composite decoder-coordinate claim is not confirmed), and then freezes in both (descriptively so in decoder coordinates; angular steps 52.9 to 1.2 degrees over eight iterations), while subsequent displacement is predominantly radial (0.961 of squared-norm) and readout-null to a measured bound (radial logit effect <=5.7e-6). An earlier velocity-trough claim is withdrawn: pre-registered normalization controls showed it coordinate-dependent. Intermediates were never recoverable through the tied readout (relay 0.00). All criteria were frozen before their data; the predictions ledger, including this paper's own withdrawn headline, ships in the companion repository.

</details>


### [86] [Noise-Aware Shrinkage for Differentially Private Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03277)
*Lele Zheng,Weifeng Kong,Xinyi Zhang,Ke Cheng,Tao Zhang,Yulong Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGE是一种噪声感知的自适应收缩方法，用于差分隐私零阶优化（DP-ZO），通过根据估计的信号质量自适应衰减私有化梯度估计，在不消耗额外隐私预算和模型查询的情况下提升大型语言模型微调的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于聚合的DP-ZO方法以固定尺度重建模型更新，忽略了训练过程中有用信号强度是变化的这一事实，导致噪声主导的更新可能被赋予过大权重而降低模型效用。

Method: SAGE通过以下方式实现噪声感知收缩：从观测二阶矩中减去已知高斯噪声方差以估计底层信号能量；通过时间追踪稳定该估计；将当前信噪比与预热参考值比较以导出有界收缩因子。该方法作为纯后处理步骤，不增加隐私预算和模型查询，仅引入常数级额外状态。

Result: 理论分析证明收缩使二次更新风险项的下降速度快于线性下降项，在保留有效下降的同时限制噪声主导更新的影响。在RoBERTa-large、OPT-1.3B和OPT-6.7B上的实验表明，SAGE在相同隐私预算下大多数设置优于现有基线，同时保持DP-ZO的前向只读内存效率。

Conclusion: SAGE有效解决了DP-ZO中固定尺度更新导致噪声更新权重过大的问题，通过自适应收缩在保持内存效率的同时提升了私有微调的性能，且不增加额外隐私成本。

Abstract: Differentially private zeroth-order optimization (DP-ZO) enables memory-efficient private fine-tuning of large language models using only forward evaluations. Existing aggregation-based DP-ZO methods reconstruct model updates at a fixed scale, ignoring that the strength of useful signals varies throughout training. Consequently, noise-dominated updates may receive excessive weight and degrade model utility. To address this issue, we propose SAGE, a noise-aware shrinkage method that adaptively attenuates privatized estimates according to their estimated signal quality. SAGE subtracts the known Gaussian noise variance from the observed second moment to estimate the underlying signal energy, stabilizes this estimate through temporal tracking, and compares its current signal-to-noise level with a warm-up reference to derive a bounded shrinkage factor. As pure post-processing, SAGE requires neither additional privacy budget nor model queries and introduces only constant additional state. Our theoretical analysis shows that shrinkage reduces the quadratic update-risk term faster than the linear descent term, preserving useful descent while limiting the influence of noise-dominated updates. Experiments on RoBERTa-large, OPT-1.3B, and OPT-6.7B demonstrate that SAGE outperforms existing baselines in most settings under the same privacy budgets while preserving the forward-only memory efficiency of DP-ZO.

</details>


### [87] [The Tell-Tale Trace: Detecting Reasoning Failures in LLMs Using Chain-of-Thought Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03291)
*Shashwat Sourav,Aishwarya Balwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过可观测思维链（CoT）的动态结构特征来区分大语言模型的推理成功与失败，而不依赖于对每个陈述语义真实性的假设。在可验证的布尔可满足性（SAT/UNSAT）任务上，研究发现失败轨迹表现出"过早验证崩溃"和"过早下结论"等系统性结构异常，并据此设计提示干预，将Llama3-70B的准确率从13.3%提升至85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于CoT的推理监控方法主要评估单个中间步骤的语义正确性或一致性，忽略了推理过程在整条轨迹上的演化动态。此外，口头化的CoT不一定忠实反映模型的内部推理，因此需要一种不依赖语义忠实性的分析方法，来系统地诊断和纠正跨推理轨迹分布的失败。

Method: 在可验证的布尔可满足性问题（SAT/UNSAT）上测试多种LLM，在每条链的能力边界附近进行受控比较。对CoT中的每个句子按推理功能进行标注（如子句检查、候选赋值等），分析成功与失败轨迹在推理动力学上的系统性差异，包括过早进入验证阶段、重复相似操作、提前终止等模式。然后基于这些发现设计针对性的证明搜索提示干预。

Result: 在SAT问题上，错误轨迹表现出"过早验证崩溃"：更早进入子句检查、重复类似操作、更早完成。在UNSAT问题上，模型过早转向错误的SAT结论，检查候选赋值而不是推导矛盾。设计的提示干预将Llama3-70B的准确率从13.3%提升至85%，纠正了84.6%的错误。

Conclusion: 推理能力失败可以表现为分布在整体推理轨迹上的、任务依赖的结构性变化。即使不对口头化CoT是否忠实反映模型内部计算做出假设，仅依靠CoT的动态特征也能帮助诊断和纠正推理失败。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning improves large language model (LLM) performance while also providing an observable interface to the model's reasoning process. Existing approaches that leverage verbalized CoTs to monitor reasoning correctness, however, largely evaluate the semantic correctness or consistency of individual intermediate steps, rather than how the reasoning process evolves across the trace. As a result, failures distributed across the reasoning trajectory, rather than those localized to a single incorrect step, remain comparatively underexplored. Furthermore, verbalized CoTs need not faithfully reflect the model's internal reasoning, motivating analyses that do not treat individual statements as literal accounts of internal computation. In this work, we therefore ask whether the dynamics of visible CoT can be leveraged to systematically distinguish successful from failed reasoning without assuming such semantic faithfulness. We study a range of LLMs on verifiable Boolean satisfiability tasks with variable complexity, enabling controlled comparisons near each model's capability frontier. Tagging CoT sentences by reasoning function reveals premature verification collapse on SAT problems: incorrect traces enter clause checking earlier, repeat similar operations, and finalize sooner. On UNSAT problems, models presumptuously move towards incorrect SAT conclusions, checking candidate assignments rather than deriving contradictions across constructed cases. Subsequently, a targeted proof-search prompt intervention raises Llama3-70B accuracy from 13.3% to 85%, correcting 84.6% of these errors. These results show that capability failures can manifest as distributed, task-dependent changes in the structure of visible reasoning, and that CoT dynamics agnostic to whether the verbalized trace reflects the model's internal computations can help diagnose and correct failures.

</details>


### [88] [Provably Learning Multi-Head Attention with Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03294)
*Sunyeop Kim,Insung Kim,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从黑盒输入输出中学习多头softmax注意力机制的问题。提出了一种无需子空间假设的算法，通过合并具有相同W_h的注意力头并对其v_h求和（当和为零时丢弃该合并头），利用改变token副本数量的插值技术来恢复规范表示。在精确计算下，该算法以概率1恢复规范对{(W_h,v_h)}（允许置换），已知H时使用4Hd²-2H+1次值查询，仅知上界H₀时使用4H₀d²-2H₀+1次查询。对于近似输出，给出了参数误差与输出误差的常数倍数关系，并扩展至一层Transformer（多头注意力加无偏ReLU前馈网络）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单头softmax注意力恢复算法需要O(d²)次值查询，但对多头情况要求头部占据成对正交子空间且需已知子空间基。本文旨在去除这些限制性假设，在更一般的条件下实现多头注意力的可识别性与参数恢复。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 通过合并相同W_h的头并将v_h求和，若和为零则丢弃，得到规范表示；(2) 通过改变token副本数量，获得一个有理函数的样本，通过插值分离规范头；(3) 添加选定token向量构造额外查询以匹配不同查询中的同一头；(4) 随机选择查询向量，在精确条件下以概率1恢复规范对，并给出精确查询复杂度上界；对近似情形给出误差传播界；最后扩展至带无偏ReLU前馈网络的一层Transformer。

Result: 算法在已知H时用4Hd²-2H+1次值查询（最大长度2H+1）以概率1恢复规范参数对（允许置换）；仅知上界H₀时使用4H₀d²-2H₀+1次查询（最大长度2H₀+1）。对近似输出，参数误差不超过模型和查询相关的常数倍输出误差。算法扩展至一层Transformer（多头注意力+无偏ReLU前馈网络），可在无需单独学习前馈网络的情况下恢复功能等价的Transformer。

Conclusion: 本文去除了此前多头注意力恢复所需的子空间正交和基已知假设，提出了一种通过规范化和有理函数插值实现多头softmax注意力参数恢复的通用算法，并给出了精确及近似场景下的理论保证，同时将其推广到带ReLU前馈网络的一层Transformer。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning multi-head softmax attention from black-box input-output access. The learner may query arbitrary real-valued token sequences and observe only the scalar output at the final token. Recent work gives an algorithm using $O(d^2)$ value queries to recover the single-head parameters $(W,v)$. For multiple heads, the same work establishes identifiability under the assumption that the heads occupy pairwise orthogonal subspaces. Applying the single-head recovery algorithm separately to the heads additionally requires bases for these subspaces to be known. We recover a canonical representation by merging heads with the same $W_h$, summing their corresponding $v_h$, and discarding a merged head when this sum is zero, without these subspace assumptions. By varying the number of copies of a token, our algorithm obtains samples of a rational function whose interpolation separates the canonical heads. Additional queries formed by adding selected token vectors then match the same head across different queries. When the oracle outputs and all subsequent computations are exact, the learner chooses its query vectors at random and recovers the canonical pairs $\{(W_h,v_h):h\in[H]\}$ up to permutation with probability one. When $H$ is known, it uses exactly $4Hd^2-2H+1$ value queries of maximum length $2H+1$. If only a known upper bound $H_0$ is available, the algorithm uses $4H_0d^2-2H_0+1$ value queries of maximum length $2H_0+1$. For approximate oracle outputs, we give conditions under which the parameter error is at most a model- and query-dependent constant multiple of the output error. Finally, we extend our result to a one-layer Transformer with multi-head attention followed by a bias-free ReLU feed-forward network. Under additional conditions, we recover a functionally equivalent Transformer without relying on a separate algorithm for learning the feed-forward network.

</details>


### [89] [Any-OPD: Heterogeneous On-Policy Distillation for Flow-Matching Models via Representation-Space Bridging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03316)
*Siming Fu,Zheming Fu,Ruizhe He,Hualiang Wang,Jie Huang,Xiaoxiao Ma,Mingchen Zhong,Weihu Huang,Xiaoxuan He,Haojun Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Any-OPD，首个支持任意模型对之间进行on-policy蒸馏的框架，通过将教师视为黑盒采样器，仅在冻结的模型无关视觉表征空间中连接两个模型，解决了跨模型家族蒸馏时latent坐标系、架构和时间步网格不匹配的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的on-policy蒸馏方法隐含假设教师和学生共享相同的VAE latent空间、相似的架构以及一致的时间步网格。当最强教师与学生来自不同模型家族（如FLUX和SD3.5）时，这些假设全部失效：教师latent无法作为异坐标系中的目标，逐像素损失会退化为模糊或发散，时间步索引在调度不匹配时失去意义。标准方案无法解决这一实际需求。

Method: Any-OPD将教师完全视为黑盒采样器，只在单一连接点上关联两个模型：一个冻结的、模型无关的视觉表征空间（如PickScore或HPSv3等质量指标对应的表示），在其中比较各自独立解码输出。轨迹对应通过匹配连续噪声水平而非离散步索引来恢复；此外引入简短的锚定阶段，将教师样本通过学生自己的VAE重新编码，确保on-policy梯度度量的是样本质量而非域不匹配。

Result: 将12B的FLUX.1-dev蒸馏到2.5B的SD3.5-Medium，Any-OPD将学生的PickScore从0.846提升至0.884，HPSv3从9.12提升至10.97，在仅为教师五分之一规模的情况下接近教师水平，而直接latent回归方法完全无法训练。

Conclusion: Any-OPD证明了跨异构模型家族的on-policy蒸馏是可行的，通过模型无关的视觉表征空间和连续噪声水平对齐，可有效将大规模教师模型的知识迁移到不同架构的小规模学生模型，为跨家族蒸馏提供了一种通用的解决方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation, in which a teacher corrects samples that the student itself generates, presupposes that the two models speak the same language: identical VAE latents, matching architectures, and a common timestep grid. We ask what happens when none of this holds, as when the strongest teacher available and the student one wishes to deploy come from different model families, and find that the standard recipes have no answer: teacher latents cannot serve as targets in a foreign coordinate system, per-pixel losses against a teacher that stochastically re-draws local detail degenerate into blur or divergence, and timestep indices lose their meaning across mismatched schedules. We present Any-OPD, to our knowledge the first framework for on-policy distillation between arbitrary pairs of latent flow-matching generators. Any-OPD treats the teacher purely as a black-box sampler and connects the two models at exactly one point: a frozen, model-agnostic vision representation in which their independently decoded outputs are compared, sidestepping every assumption about latents, features, or architecture. Trajectory correspondence is recovered by matching continuous noise levels instead of step indices, and a brief anchoring phase, in which teacher samples are re-encoded through the student's own VAE, ensures the on-policy gradient measures sample quality rather than domain mismatch. Distilling the 12B FLUX.1-dev into the 2.5B SD3.5-Medium, Any-OPD lifts the student's PickScore from 0.846 to 0.884 and HPSv3 from 9.12 to 10.97, rivaling the teacher at a fifth of its size, where direct latent regression fails to train at all.

</details>


### [90] [AS-FedBridge: Pseudo-Spike Bridge Distillation for Heterogeneous ANN-SNN Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03324)
*Shengyang Li,Yiting Dong,Liuyang Song,Ximing Wang,Luyuan Xie,Cong Li,Qingni Shen,Zhaofei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AS-FedBridge，一种面向混合ANN-SNN客户端的联邦学习框架，通过轻量级Bridge中的Pseudo-Spike Interface将连续信号投影到尖峰兼容空间，解决ANN与SNN之间的表征不对齐问题，在四个数据集上实现了先进精度并保持了低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习结合SNN可实现资源受限边缘设备上的高能效分布式训练，但ANN与SNN联合训练时存在表征不对齐问题——连续实值激活与离散时空尖峰之间存在语义鸿沟，这阻碍了混合ANN-SNN客户端在联邦学习中的协同效果。

Method: 提出AS-FedBridge框架，包含一个轻量级Bridge，其核心是Pseudo-Spike Interface，能将ANN的连续信号投影到尖峰兼容的空间中，实现ANN-SNN之间的表征对齐，从而支持混合客户端场景下的联邦协同训练。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验表明，ANN-SNN对齐程度与联邦学习性能呈正相关；AS-FedBridge在保持先进准确率的同时，有效应对了极端的规模、架构和客户端异构性挑战，且仅引入边际计算开销，并提供了模型性能与资源效率之间高度可控的权衡。

Conclusion: AS-FedBridge为混合ANN-SNN联邦学习系统建立了稳健且实用的基础，通过解决表征不对齐问题实现了高精度、低开销的跨架构联邦协同训练。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training across distributed edge devices while strictly preserving data privacy. To facilitate practical deployment on resource-constrained edge devices, Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have emerged as a promising alternative to traditional Artificial Neural Networks (ANNs) due to their sparse computing mechanisms and high energy efficiency. However, jointly training ANNs and SNNs exposes a challenge of representational misalignment, which is intrinsically caused by differences in information representation, specifically the semantic gap between continuous real-valued activations in ANNs and discrete spatio-temporal spikes in SNNs. To overcome this barrier, we propose AS-FedBridge, a novel federated learning framework tailored for mixed ANN-SNN clients. AS-FedBridge features a lightweight Bridge equipped with a Pseudo-Spike Interface, which effectively projects continuous signals into a spike-compatible space to facilitate ANN-SNN alignment. Given the absence of existing mixed ANN-SNN federated frameworks, we establish a comprehensive benchmark to evaluate against multiple advanced heterogeneous FL methods. Our empirical analysis demonstrates a positive correlation between the degree of ANN-SNN alignment and the collaborative FL performance. Across four datasets, AS-FedBridge consistently demonstrates advanced accuracy while mitigating extreme scale, architecture, and client heterogeneity challenge. Furthermore, our framework enables a highly controllable trade-off between model performance and resource efficiency. AS-FedBridge accomplishes these robust performance gains while introducing only marginal computational overhead, establishing a robust and practical foundation for mixed ANN-SNN federated learning systems.

</details>


### [91] [Tight Worst-Case Bounds for the Smallest Eigenvalue of ReLU NTK Gram Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03368)
*Zhao Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了n个单位向量的连续ReLU导数Gram矩阵H的最小特征值下界。通过对两两内积在标准高斯方向下取平均，证明了最小特征值的遍历维数无关下界Ω(Δ±/√log n)，并构造最坏情形族证明该界在常数意义下紧确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究ReLU导数Gram矩阵的特征值性质对于理解神经网络中ReLU激活函数的几何与谱性质具有重要意义。特别是在高维空间中，需要确定该矩阵最小特征值的下界，以刻画向量集合的结构性质。

Method: ①定义连续ReLU导数Gram矩阵H，其元素通过对高斯方向上的成对门控内积取平均得到；②引入投影分离量Δ±刻画向量间的距离特征；③证明最小特征值的通用维数无关下界λ_min(H)=Ω(Δ±/√log n)；④构造最坏情形族，证明匹配的上界λ_min(H)=O(Δ±/√log n)，从而验证所证明下界的紧性。

Result: 本文证明了对于任意n个单位向量，连续ReLU导数Gram矩阵的最小特征值满足维数无关的通用下界λ_min(H)=Ω(Δ±/√log n)。同时通过构造最坏情形案例家族，证明了该下界在常数因子意义下是紧确的，即达到匹配的上界。

Conclusion: 本文建立了连续ReLU导数Gram矩阵特征值的紧确谱下界，其依赖仅体现在投影分离量和√log n因子，与维数d无关。这一结果在通用常数意义下是最优的，为ReLU神经网络的理论分析提供了关键的几何工具。

Abstract: For $n$ unit vectors $x_1,\ldots,x_n \in \mathbb{R}^d$, we study the continuous ReLU derivative Gram matrix $H$, whose entries are obtained by averaging pairwise gated inner products over a standard Gaussian direction. Writing $ Δ_\pm := \min_{i \neq j} \min\{ \|x_i-x_j\|_2, \|x_i+x_j\|_2 \} $ for their projective separation, we prove the universal dimension-free lower bound $ λ_{\min}(H) = Ω( Δ_\pm/\sqrt{\log n} ) $. Conversely, we construct worst-case families satisfying the matching upper bound $ λ_{\min}(H) = O( Δ_\pm/\sqrt{\log n} ) $, showing that this rate is tight up to universal constants.

</details>


### [92] [Benign interpolation and Occam's razor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03386)
*Tom F. Sterkenburg,Daniel A. Herrmann,Jan-Willem Romeijn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从哲学视角审视深度学习中的良性插值现象（benign interpolation），指出新兴的解释性理论诉诸"简约偏好"（simplicity preference），但这一简约概念缺乏与泛化之间的可证明联系，与传统统计学习理论中通过定理衔接模型类简约性与泛化性能的做法形成对比，从而产生了解释上的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法在完美拟合训练数据时仍能良好泛化（良性插值），这无法由经典统计学习理论解释，催生了大量新的解释尝试。作者旨在面向哲学读者澄清这一争论，并揭示新提案中诉诸简约性的逻辑缺陷。

Method: 文章采用哲学分析的方法，对比经典统计学习理论中"简约性通过定理与泛化相连接"的论证路径，与新近解释中"以个体模型属性充当简约性"的论证路径，指出后者的简约性命名难以替代定理所提供的实质性保证。

Result: 研究发现：新解释诉诸的"简约性"并非经典理论意义上具有可证明泛化保证的模型类简单性，而是对个体模型属性的合理化解构。由于缺乏可证明的联系，"简约性"这一命名承担了原本由定理承担的论证工作，使得一个实质性但未经验证的假设看起来像是应用了熟悉的方法论原则。

Conclusion: 作者主张解释深度学习泛化需要一个明确的简约性概念，并且该概念需要具备与泛化之间的可证明联系；否则，仅凭命名上的"简约"无法构成充分的解释。

Abstract: Contemporary deep learning methods generalize well even when they fit their training data perfectly, a phenomenon known as benign interpolation. This phenomenon cannot be accounted for by classical statistical learning theory and has prompted a range of attempted new explanations in the statistics and machine learning literature. A common feature of these new proposals is an appeal to a simplicity preference among interpolating models, often presented as a form of Occam's razor. We clarify this debate for a philosophical audience and argue that this new appeal to simplicity creates an explanatory gap. The classical theory offers theorems which connect the simplicity of model classes to good generalization, thus underwriting methodological simplicity norms. The new accounts instead appeal to properties of individual models, which they interpret as a kind of simplicity. Lacking a provable connection to generalization, it is the name "simplicity" that does the work a theorem used to do, making a substantive and unargued assumption look like the application of a familiar methodological principle.

</details>


### [93] [TimeRLM: Recursive Language Models Enable Precise Anomaly Localization in Long-Context Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03391)
*Nicolas Zumarraga,Lorenzo Steno,Ning Wang,Max Rosenblattl,Thomas Kaar,Maxwell A. Xu,Kevin O'Sullivan,Markus Kreft,Elgar Fleisch,Paul Schmiedmayer,Patrick Langer,Robert Jakob*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeRLM，一个面向时间序列的递归语言模型框架，通过代码和视觉能力顺序操作信号来处理长上下文时间序列中的异常定位问题。同时引入AnomalyXL合成基准，在多数任务上显著超越现有TSLM和单次传递基线，并通过强化学习后训练进一步提升性能与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文时间序列中的精确异常定位是临床护理、工业运营、金融服务和物流等监控应用中的关键任务。尽管TSLM能摄取时间序列数据并以自然语言描述异常发现，但近期基准显示其在长上下文下的检索性能下降。受文本领域递归语言模型（RLM）通过外部上下文和代码查询恢复性能的启发，作者希望将这一思路推广到时间序列领域。

Method: 1）提出TimeRLM，一种递归语言模型（RLM）框架，通过代码和视觉能力顺序操作时间序列信号，保持上下文在LLM外部。2）构建AnomalyXL，一个合成长上下文异常定位基准，包含程序化注入的异常，涵盖五个任务类别和两个变体（AnomalyXL-MCQ和AnomalyXL-Localize）。3）对TimeRLM使用强化学习进行后训练，减少智能体交互轮数并提升性能。

Result: TimeRLM在AnomalyXL-Localize的五个任务中四个上超越所有评估的TSLM和单次传递基线，定位IoU达0.682，分类带证据达0.745，而基线最高仅为0.329和0.072。经过强化学习后训练的模型性能进一步提升，交互轮数减少约三分之二。在未见过的真实世界ECG、睡眠和软件可观测性数据上，后训练的TimeRLM保持或提升性能，即使仅用合成数据训练也超越TSLM。

Conclusion: 递归交互式处理时间序列是长时域检索的有效方法。TimeRLM展示了将上下文外部化并通过代码查询的方式在时间序列领域同样有效，为长上下文时间序列异常定位提供了新范式。

Abstract: Precise anomaly localization over long-context time series is a crucial task in monitoring applications across clinical care, industrial operations, financial services, and logistics, where brief evidence may hide inside long spans of high-frequency data. Time-Series Language Models (TSLMs) are able to ingest time series data and verbalize findings on anomalies in natural language; however, recent benchmarks report a decrease in retrieval performance at long contexts, mirroring failure modes in text, vision, and audio. In the text domain, Recursive Language Models (RLMs) can recover much of this lost performance by keeping context external to the large language model (LLM), allowing the model to query it through code. We present TimeRLM, an RLM formulation for time-series that sequentially manipulates the signal using code and vision capabilities. We further introduce AnomalyXL, a synthetic long-context anomaly localization benchmark with programmatically injected anomalies that require precise retrieval. We implement five different task categories and two variants: AnomalyXL-MCQ and AnomalyXL-Localize. TimeRLM outperforms every evaluated TSLM and single-pass baseline on four of the five AnomalyXL-Localize tasks, reaching 0.682 IoU on localization and 0.745 on classify-with-evidence, versus at most 0.329 and 0.072 across all baselines. We post-train TimeRLM using reinforcement learning. The resulting model further improves performance and requires approximately one-third as many agent interaction turns as its untrained base model to produce a final answer. On unseen real-world ECG, sleep and software observability recordings, the post-trained TimeRLM retains or improves performance, surpassing TSLMs despite being trained exclusively on synthetic data. Our findings suggest recursive interaction with time-series is an effective approach for long-horizon retrieval.

</details>


### [94] [Shorter Reasoning, Earlier Answers? An Evaluation of Reasoning Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03401)
*Francesca Carlon,Vincent Ginis,Andres Algaba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了在不同推理预算（token上限）下，减少大语言模型推理长度的策略对准确率的影响。使用198个GPQA Diamond和500个MMLU-Pro问题，测试了Qwen3-14B的简洁提示和gpt-oss系列的训练努力设置。结果显示，简短推理的收益主要来自"提前完成"而非"更好的中间答案"，并建议评估报告应区分截止前正确完成、中途停止的答案、未完成运行差异及概率质量等维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在回答前往往进行冗长推理，增加成本和延迟。虽然可以通过提示或训练设置缩短推理，但仅观察更短的推理轨迹无法判断模型是真的提前得出正确答案，还是仅仅提前停止推理（可能尚未完成思考）。需要一个在匹配的推理深度（token预算）下对比不同策略的评估框架。

Method: 1. 使用198个GPQA Diamond和500个MMLU-Pro问题，进行配对运行（同一问题在匹配的推理token预算下对比不同设置）。2. 测试三类缩短推理的方法：(a) Qwen3-14B的数值/简洁提示（宣布token限制），(b) gpt-oss-20b和-120b的训练努力设置（low/medium/high）。3. 对比不同token预算下的准确率、完成状态（finished/unfinished）、候选logit答案及概率质量。

Result: 1. Qwen简洁提示缩短推理12-17%，但在匹配token限制下准确率变化小且方向不一。2. 简洁/早答指令在512 token下使MMLU-Pro准确率提升3.8个百分点（其中2.7点来自两个运行都未完成时），但在2048 token下收益不确定。3. gpt-oss中，低/中努力从完成推理获得的候选logit答案比匹配token预算下的高努力答案准确14.5-26.3个百分点；512-token优势主要来自低努力更早完成，而均未完成时的差异较小且混合。4. 错误的早期答案往往将所有概率集中在所选选项上，说明提前停止并不统一改善概率质量。

Conclusion: 严格的推理截止时间有利于低努力设置或简洁指令，但如果允许高努力完成推理，可以获得更高的最终准确率。评估实践应分别报告以下指标：截止前正确完成的准确率、运行被停止时的答案、未完成运行之间的差异、以及赋予正确答案的概率。

Abstract: Large language models often reason at length before answering, increasing cost and latency. Prompts and trained settings can shorten this reasoning, but a shorter trace may only show that the model stopped sooner. Here, we evaluate paired runs of the same question at matched reasoning horizons across 198 GPQA Diamond and 500 MMLU-Pro questions. We test a numeric/concision prompt that announces a token limit for Qwen3-14B and the trained effort settings of gpt-oss-20b and -120b. The Qwen prompt shortens reasoning traces by 12-17%, while accuracy changes at matched token limits are small and mixed. A concise/early-answer instruction raises MMLU-Pro accuracy by 3.8 percentage points at 512 tokens, including +2.7 points when both runs are unfinished. Its gain at 2,048 tokens is uncertain. For gpt-oss, candidate-logit answers from completed low- and medium-effort reasoning are 14.5-26.3 points more accurate than matched-horizon high-effort answers. Most of the 512-token advantage comes from lower effort finishing earlier, while differences among unfinished runs are smaller and mixed. Wrong early answers often concentrate probability on the chosen option, so earlier stopping does not uniformly improve probability quality. In these tests, a tight deadline can favor lower effort or a concise instruction, whereas allowing high effort to finish can recover higher final accuracy. Evaluations should report correct completion before a deadline, the answer obtained when a run is stopped, differences among unfinished runs, and probability assigned to the correct answer separately.

</details>


### [95] [Stop Replacing Noise with Noise: Two-Source Reliability Assessment for Label Correction and Sample Reweighting in Label-Noise Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03432)
*Wenxiao Fan,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，用于解耦噪声标注学习中的双源可靠性评估，分别评估观测标签和伪目标的可信度，避免二者互补耦合带来的噪声传播问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于修复的噪声标签学习方法（refurbishment-based）通常用一个样本级清洁度分数同时控制观测标签和伪目标两个分支，这造成隐式耦合：降低对观测标签的信任会自动增加对伪目标的信任。但伪目标本身可能从损坏的监督中学习了噪声，从而以一个不可靠信号替代另一个不可靠信号。作者通过表征诊断发现，噪声监督对深层特征影响更强，而浅层关系相对稳定且提供了超越损失后验的额外信息，这启发了双源解耦评估的必要性。

Method: TRACE框架包含两个独立的可靠性评估源：1）观测标签侧，综合损失拟合（loss fit）、浅层关系稳定性（shallow relation stability）和预测一致性（prediction agreement）三个维度来评估；2）伪目标侧，采用模型置信度（model confidence）单独评估。两个来源各自的分数分别控制标签修正和目标监督强度，不假设二者可靠性互补。

Result: 在合成和真实世界的噪声基准数据集上，TRACE显著提升了多种代表性修复方法（refurbishment baselines）的性能，同时生成了更可靠的伪监督信号。

Conclusion: TRACE通过解耦观测标签与伪目标的双源可靠性评估，有效缓解了修复式噪声学习中的互补耦合问题，验证了浅层关系稳定性作为补充信号的实用价值，为噪声标签学习提供了更可靠的框架。

Abstract: Refurbishment-based noisy-label learning mixes an observed label with a model-derived pseudo target, typically using one sample-wise cleanliness score to control both branches. This creates a hidden coupling: reducing trust in the observed label automatically increases trust in the pseudo target. We show that this complementarity can replace one unreliable signal with another because a pseudo target learned from corrupted supervision may reproduce the noise it is meant to correct. Our representation diagnostics provide a consistent account of this mismatch: noisy supervision redirects deeper layers more strongly, whereas shallower relations remain comparatively stable and provide information beyond the loss posterior. We therefore propose TRACE, a Two-Source Reliability Assessment framework for Label Correction and Sample Reweighting. TRACE assesses the observed label using loss fit, shallow relation stability, and prediction agreement, while separately assessing the pseudo target using model confidence. Its source-specific scores control target correction and supervision strength without assuming complementary reliability. Across synthetic and real-world noisy benchmarks, TRACE improves representative refurbishment baselines and yields more reliable pseudo supervision.

</details>


### [96] [Approximate Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03447)
*Yuannuo Feng,Zegang Peng,Yuxin Xie,Yubing Ye,Yizhe Chen,Wenshuai Yao,Wenyong Zhou,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出近似投机解码(ASD)，一种无需训练的验证器，通过预算化的最长前缀选择替代二值的首个不匹配截断，接受选定的不匹配令牌并重用连续的目标贪心后缀，在保持相对完整性校验的同时提升吞吐量3.05%-15.26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贪心验证会在第一个与目标模型argmax不同的草稿令牌处停止解码，丢弃剩余的目标评分后缀。如果接受这种不匹配，虽然会改变解码轨迹，但在已实现的前缀下，连续的后缀可能仍然是目标贪心的，从而可以被重用，这意味着存在未被利用的效率提升空间。

Method: ASD是训练免费的验证器，将二值首错截断替换为预算化最长前缀选择。其核心机制包括：局部目标logit遗憾门控接受选定的不匹配；每块异常上限；持久的请求级遗憾预算。然后重用连续的目标贪心后缀，无需额外近似决策或目标模型前向传播。预算为零时ASD精确退化为标准贪心验证。

Result: 实验表明ASD在固定工作负载下相较于匹配的严格验证提升吞吐量3.05%-15.26%，在七个Qwen3-14B+DSpark-14B任务上平均提高7.78%。在DeepSeek-V4-Flash(284B)搭配DSpark时，在FP4转FP8兼容设置下GSM8K和MATH-500上验证端接受率提高约10%-16%。

Conclusion: ASD通过接受选定的不匹配令牌并重用连续贪心后缀，在不需新草稿模型或微调的情况下有效提升投机解码的吞吐量，且当预算为零时精确收敛为标准贪心验证，保证了方法的普适性和可控性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by verifying a draft block with a target model in parallel. Under standard greedy verification, decoding stops at the first draft token that differs from the target argmax, discarding the remaining target-scored suffix. Although accepting such a mismatch changes the decoding trajectory, it can make a contiguous suffix reusable when its tokens remain target-greedy under the realized prefix. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{Approximate Speculative Decoding (ASD)}, a training-free verifier that replaces binary first-mismatch truncation with budgeted longest-prefix selection. ASD accepts selected mismatches subject to a local target-logit regret gate, a per-block exception cap, and a persistent request-level regret budget, then reuses the contiguous target-greedy suffix without additional approximate decisions or target-model forward passes. ASD requires neither a new draft model nor fine-tuning, and exactly reduces to standard greedy verification when the budget is zero. Experiments show that ASD improves fixed-workload throughput by $3.05\%$--$15.26\%$ over matched strict verification and averages a $7.78\%$ gain across seven Qwen3-14B + DSpark-14B tasks. On DeepSeek-V4-Flash (284B) with DSpark it also raises verifier-side acceptance by roughly $10\%$--$16\%$ on GSM8K and MATH-500 in an FP4-to-FP8 compatibility setting. The source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/Kissmetothemoon/ASD

</details>


### [97] [FedCARE: A Multi-Objective Personalised Federated Learning Framework for Smart Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03498)
*Rojalini Tripathy,Padmalochan Bera,Shreya Ghosh,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedCARE框架，一种面向智能医疗服务的多目标个性化联邦学习框架，通过两阶段训练策略（Pareto驱动的多目标联邦优化学习共享骨干 + 客户端本地微调）同时解决数据异构、临床目标异构和特征空间部分重叠三大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法无法同时处理医疗场景中三个关键挑战：1）非IID数据分布；2）不同医院具有异构且可能相互冲突的临床目标（如死亡风险预测、再入院预测、住院时长估计）；3）部分重叠的特征空间（各机构保留无法共享的私有临床特征）。现有个性化FL方法主要处理统计异构，多目标FL方法通常学习全局共享模型而缺乏客户端级自适应。

Method: FedCARE采用两阶段训练策略：第一阶段，利用Pareto驱动的多目标联邦优化从公共临床特征中学习共享全局骨干网络；第二阶段，每个客户端利用其私有特征和本地临床目标独立微调共享骨干网络，实现机构特异的个性化而不引入额外通信开销。系统部署于墨尔本研究云（Melbourne Research Cloud）上的云端客户端-服务器联邦架构。

Result: 在两个真实医疗数据集（MIMIC-III和Diabetes 130-US Hospitals）上的实验表明，FedCARE在标准FL、多目标FL和个性化FL基线对比中始终表现最优，相比FedAvg实现最高12.5%的AUROC提升和32.0%的MAE降低。

Conclusion: FedCARE有效验证了两阶段策略在解决医疗联邦学习中目标冲突与特征空间异构问题的可行性，通过共享骨干学习与本地个性化微调的结合，在保护数据隐私的同时获得了显著优于现有方法的性能，为智能医疗服务的多目标个性化联邦学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed healthcare institutions without centralising sensitive patient data. However, real-world healthcare federations are often characterised not only by non-IID data, but also by heterogeneous clinical objectives and partially overlapping feature spaces. Different hospitals may optimise distinct and potentially conflicting objectives, such as mortality risk prediction, readmission reduction, or length-of-stay estimation, while also retaining institution-specific clinical features that cannot be shared with other participants. Existing personalised FL methods mainly address statistical heterogeneity, whereas multi-objective FL approaches typically learn a shared global model without explicit client-level adaptation. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{FedCARE}, a multi-objective personalised FL framework for smart healthcare services. FedCARE follows a two-stage training strategy. First, it learns a shared global backbone from common clinical features using Pareto-driven multi-objective federated optimisation. Second, each client independently fine-tunes the shared backbone using its private features and local clinical objectives, enabling institution-specific personalisation without additional communication overhead. We implement FedCARE in a cloud-based client-server federated deployment on the Melbourne Research Cloud and evaluate it on two real-world healthcare datasets, MIMIC-III and Diabetes 130-US Hospitals. Experimental results show that FedCARE consistently outperforms standard FL, multi-objective FL, and personalised FL baselines, achieving up to 12.5% AUROC improvement and 32.0% MAE reduction over FedAvg.

</details>


### [98] [Robust General Utility for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03562)
*Zixuan Liu,Fangzheng Wu,Brian Summa,Zizhan Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出鲁棒通用效用强化学习（robust general-utility RL），一种在效用不确定集内训练策略以应对效用错配的极小极大学习框架，并针对凹与非凹效用分别设计了可证明收敛的随机算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往通用效用强化学习假设评估效用固定且正确指定，但实际部署时效用可能与训练时存在偏差，造成先前工作未解决的鲁棒性缺口。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出应对效用错配的鲁棒学习框架。

Method: 提出鲁棒通用效用RL极小极大框架，通过选择效用不确定集统一涵盖标准通用效用RL、奖励鲁棒RL和约束RL等现有框架；针对凹效用设计投影随机梯度下降-上升（projected stochastic gradient descent-ascent）方法并获得平稳性保证；针对更困难非凹效用提出随机prox-extragradient算法以缓解非凹导致的病态行为，并保证收敛到近似一阶平稳点。

Result: 对所提两类算法分别建立了收敛性（平稳性与近似一阶平稳性）保证；在LLM安全对齐和探索最大化任务上的实验验证了与理论一致的收敛行为。

Conclusion: 鲁棒通用效用RL严格推广了标准通用效用RL，并通过合适的不确定集设置统一了多个已有RL框架，为应对部署时效用错配提供了理论有保证的求解算法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with general utility extends classic RL by optimizing an arbitrary utility functional of the policy-induced occupancy measure, thereby enabling a broader range of applications. However, previous work on general utility RL typically assumes the evaluation utility is fixed and correctly specified. In practice, the utility used at deployment can deviate from the training one, creating a robustness gap that prior work does not address. Motivated by this, we propose robust general-utility RL, a minimax learning framework that trains policies against utility misspecification within a prescribed uncertainty set. Our framework strictly generalizes standard general-utility RL while also providing a unified view of many existing RL frameworks, including reward-robust RL and constrained RL, through appropriate choices of the utility uncertainty set. We further develop provably convergent stochastic algorithms for two regimes. For concave utilities, we develop a projected stochastic gradient descent-ascent method and establish stationarity guarantees. For the more challenging nonconcave regime, we propose a stochastic prox-extragradient algorithm that mitigates ill-posed behavior induced by nonconcavity, with convergence guarantees to approximate first-order stationarity. Experiments on LLM safety alignment and exploration maximization tasks further corroborate the convergence behavior consistent with our theory.

</details>


### [99] [Pin Once, Swap Light: Subspace-Aligned Centroid-Residual Training for Efficient Ultra-LoRA Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03579)
*Xiang Li,Pengcheng Wang,Huazheng Wang,Saurabh Bagchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SALT是一个面向多租户LoRA服务系统的层次化微调框架，通过联合训练高容量域质心（domain centroid）和超低秩任务残差适配器（r≤2），在保持高精度服务的同时将每适配器显存占用降低16倍，并显著提升vLLM在不同硬件约束下的服务吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多租户LoRA服务系统面临服务效率与任务性能之间的关键矛盾：高秩适配器性能更好但显存占用大且PCIe交换开销高，严重限制可扩展性；而超低秩适配器（r≤2）虽大幅降低显存与交换开销，但任务性能退化明显。

Method: SALT分为三个阶段：(1) 提供方在领域公共数据上联合训练高容量域质心，使用新颖的对齐正则化器（alignment regularizer）将领域内任务子空间凝聚为统一基；(2) 用户在冻结的质心之上对私有数据微调超低秩任务残差适配器；(3) 推理时将质心固定在GPU显存中，按需动态换入每个用户的任务残差。

Result: 在不同规模的LLM上，SALT使用r≤2的残差恢复了高秩精度，相比最先进的压缩基线最高提升18.5%的绝对准确率，每适配器显存减少最多16倍。集成到vLLM后，在PCIe带宽压力下服务吞吐量提升最高51%，在GPU显存受限条件下对Llama-3.2-3B提升28%。

Conclusion: SALT通过子空间对齐训练有效解决多租户LoRA服务中效率与性能的权衡问题，验证了共享域质心加超低秩残差的范式能够在资源受限条件下同时实现高性能和高吞吐服务。

Abstract: Modern multi-tenant Low-Rank Adapters (LoRAs) serving systems concurrently host tens to hundreds of LoRA adapters. Though powerful, this introduces a critical system dilemma between serving efficiency and task performance: higher-rank adapters generally achieve better downstream task performance, but their GPU VRAM footprint and Host-to-Device PCIe swapping overhead severely constrain scalability. Conversely, ultra-low-rank adapters ($r \le 2$) minimize both VRAM footprint and PCIe transfer overhead, but suffer from downstream task performance degradation. To solve this problem, we propose Subspace-Aligned LoRA Training (SALT), a serving efficiency-aware hierarchical fine-tuning framework. Our solution operates in three phases. First, a provider jointly trains high-capacity domain centroids on public data within the domain using a novel alignment regularizer that coheres in-domain task subspaces into a unified basis. Next, users fine-tune ultra-low-rank task residual adapters on private data atop those frozen centroids. Finally, during inference, the provider pins the centroid in GPU VRAM and dynamically swaps in each user's task residual on demand. Across LLMs of varying scales, SALT recovers high-rank accuracy using $r \le 2$ residuals, achieving up to 18.5% absolute accuracy gains over state-of-the-art compression baselines and reducing per-adapter memory by up to 16x. When integrated into vLLM, SALT improves serving throughput by up to 51% under PCIe bandwidth pressure and 28% under GPU VRAM constraints for Llama-3.2-3B.

</details>


### [100] [Design-Time Optimization of Deep Neural Networks for Intermittent Learning on Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03589)
*Jakob Schubert,Maximilian Kasper,Maximilian Linke,Benedict Herzog,Mark Deutel,Axel Plinge,Dominik Seuss,Christopher Mutschler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向间歇性、能量自主的MCU端设备上学习的DNN设计方法，通过硬件感知的能量预测模型与多目标优化（MOO）相结合，在离线设计阶段即可优化DNN架构，无需反复部署和在线测试。在Cortex-M4 MCU上以自编码器进行异常检测验证，能量预测器达到16.6%的加权绝对百分比误差，足以在间歇性约束下进行可靠的架构选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在能量可耗尽（如太阳能供电）的移动应用中，AI执行面临学习随时可能被中断的技术问题，亟需一种能够在设计阶段就考虑间歇性约束的DNN设计方法。

Method: 将硬件感知的能量预测模型与多目标优化（MOO）相结合，能量预测器基于DNN模型提取的实现特定的计算和内存特征，估算推理和训练（含间歇性检查点开销）的逐层能量消耗，实现在设计阶段的离线DNN优化。

Result: 在Cortex-M4 MCU上使用自编码器进行异常检测验证，能量预测器达到16.6%的加权绝对百分比误差，足以在间歇性约束下实现可靠的架构选择。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了MOO、自动化DNN设计、能量采集系统部署和间歇性学习之间的鸿沟，真正实现了边缘端的自主AI。

Abstract: We present a method for designing deep neural networks (DNNs) for intermittent, energy-autonomous, on-device learning on microcontroller units (MCUs). In mobile applications where the energy can run out, e.g., when solar-powered, executing artificial intelligence (AI) faces a technical issue as learning can be interrupted at any time. Our approach combines a hardware-aware energy prediction model with multi-objective optimization (MOO), enabling offline DNN optimization at the design stage without repeated deployment and online testing on the target MCU. Our proposed energy predictor estimates per-layer energy consumption for both DNN inference and training, including the intermittent checkpointing overhead, based on implementation-specific compute and memory features extracted from the DNN model. We validate our approach using autoencoders for anomaly detection on a Cortex-M4 MCU, where our predictor achieves a weighted absolute percentage error of 16.6%, which is sufficient for reliable architecture selection under intermittency constraints. As a result, this work bridges the gap between MOO, automated DNN design, deployment on energy-harvesting systems, and intermittent learning, truly enabling autonomous AI at the edge.

</details>


### [101] [A Theory of Conditional Collapse under Low-Rank Weight-Space Ablations: I. The Single-Block Theory and Synthetic Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03620)
*Abdallah Khemais*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在理想化的残差流模型中严格推导了激活修补（activation patching）与权重消融（weight-space ablation）两种因果归因方法何时一致的三条精确结果，并通过合成条件任务的Transformer实验验证了这些预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活修补和权重消融都声称一个组件对行为具有因果责任，但二者作用对象不同：一个作用于单次前向传播，另一个作用于决定每次前向传播的参数。理解这两种方法何时一致，对于可解释性和因果归因的可靠性至关重要。

Method: 构建理想化模型F(x)=F0(x)+Σαi(x)vi，其中条件计算经由残差流加性传递并由线性泛函读出。推导三条精确结果：(1)删除载体子集使匹配输入对坍缩到相同无条件输出的充要条件；(2)对比修补移动读出由对比度决定而消融由绝对水平决定，二者互不约束；(3)对注意力头与其层归一化和MLP复合推导出精确一阶交互公式，其余项为二阶且可证消失条件。

Result: 在39种消融配置下，测量交互与理想化模型预测准确性高度秩相关（Spearman -0.83）；第二个任务和架构复现相同模式并出现极性反转；单块交互结果可扩展到多个残差块。

Conclusion: 激活修补与权重消融的一致性与具体条件密切相关，两者不可互换；该理论框架为理解两种归因方法的关系提供了严格基础，并在配套论文中延伸到真实预训练模型。

Abstract: Activation patching and weight-space ablation both claim a component is causally responsible for a behavior, yet they act on different objects: one forward pass versus the parameters behind every forward pass. We ask when they agree.
  We study an idealized model where a conditional computation is carried additively through a residual stream, $F(x)=F_0(x)+\sum_iα_i(x)v_i$, read out by a linear functional, and prove three exact results. First, deleting a subset of carriers collapses a matched input pair onto the same unconditional output \emph{if and only if} the removal is symmetric on the pair and leaves no outside contrast; the error is deterministic, and we give its exact form even when the two conditions hold only approximately. Second, patching a carrier moves the readout by its donor-receiver \emph{contrast}, while ablating it moves the readout by its \emph{absolute level}; neither bounds the other, and we construct pairs where every single-carrier patch flips the decision while no single-carrier ablation does. Third, for an attention head composed with its own layer's normalization and MLP, we derive an exact first-order interaction formula with a provably second-order remainder, vanishing identically when only the MLP is ablated but not, in general, when a head is.
  Small transformers trained on a synthetic conditional task illustrate all three predictions: across thirty-nine ablation configurations the measured interaction is strongly rank-correlated with the idealized model's predictive accuracy (Spearman $-0.83$), and a second task and architecture reproduces the same pattern, including a further polarity reversal.
  The single-block interaction result extends past one residual block, and the synthetic validation is tested against a real pretrained model, in a companion paper that takes this theory further along both axes.

</details>


### [102] [ConformalShift: Targeted Event Reordering Against Adaptive ECG Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03628)
*Arash Vashagh,Yasmin Vashagh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ConformalShift攻击，通过重排真实事件的到达顺序来抑制自适应共形预测中的心室类识别，无需修改波形、标签或分类器输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应共形预测虽然能恢复点分类器遗漏的临床重要心跳类别，但其延迟反馈特性使其决策对事件到达顺序敏感。这种顺序敏感性可能被恶意利用，攻击者无需篡改任何医疗数据本身即可破坏预测系统的可靠性。

Method: ConformalShift是一种有界事件重排攻击。给定一个被选中的目标事件，攻击者在有限的置换预算内搜索可行的置换方案，重排目标之前的真实事件顺序，以降低心室类阈值，从而在目标事件被评估时抑制其心室类预测。攻击不修改波形、标签、分类器分数或事件多重集。

Result: 在MIT-BIH确认记录上，ConformalShift对Extra Trees抑制了66.7%的合格目标，对HistGradientBoosting抑制了60.0%，而随机调度基准仅为4.4%和12.0%。在INCART数据集上，迁移配置也优于随机调度。减少位移预算会削弱两个数据集上的攻击效果。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示医疗保健中的自适应监测系统可能仅通过真实信息的时序安排就被攻破，即使波形、标签、分类器输出和事件内容均保持不变。这一发现凸显了自适应共形预测在临床部署中面临的安全隐患。

Abstract: Adaptive conformal prediction can recover clinically important heartbeat classes missed by a point classifier, but delayed feedback makes its decisions sensitive to event order. We introduce ConformalShift, a bounded event-reordering attack that suppresses the ventricular class for rescued events without modifying ECG waveforms, labels, classifier scores, or the event multiset. ConformalShift searches for feasible permutations of authentic preceding events that lower the ventricular threshold before a selected target is evaluated. On disjoint MIT--BIH confirmation records, the attack suppressed 66.7% of eligible targets for Extra Trees and 60.0% for HistGradientBoosting, compared with random-schedule rates of 4.4% and 12.0%, respectively. Transferred configurations also outperformed random scheduling on INCART, while reducing the displacement budget weakened the attack on both datasets. These results show that adaptive monitors in healthcare can be compromised through the timing of authentic information, even when waveforms, labels, classifier outputs, and event contents remain unchanged.

</details>


### [103] [POEM: Phase-Aware $\mathrm{SO}(2)$ Feature Rotation for Time Series Forecasting Under Periodicity Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03630)
*Jiawen Zhu,Shuhan Liu,Shengxuan Li,Qiming Shi,Di Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出POEM，一种基于SO(2)群潜特征旋转的相位感知时间序列预测框架，通过学习相位校正坐标并应用可逆旋转来减少周期性漂移带来的相位相关变异性，实验表明其性能优越且潜轨迹更规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法在时间序列预测中取得了进展，但周期性漂移（周期时序和相位随时间变化）仍然是挑战性问题。现有方法主要在固定时间网格上建模序列，难以适应相位相关的变化。

Method: 提出POEM框架，基于二维特殊正交群SO(2)进行潜特征旋转。通过学习相位校正坐标并对成对潜特征应用可逆的SO(2)旋转来减少相位相关变异性；同时提出方向性相位增量注意力机制(DPIA)，从相似时间上下文中检索历史相位增量并将其外推集成到未来的相位校正中。

Result: 实验表明POEM取得了有竞争力的性能；定性可视化显示学习的相位感知变换使潜轨迹更加规则。

Conclusion: 基于SO(2)旋转的相位感知方式能有效处理时间序列预测中的周期性漂移问题，使模型在固定时间网格上的表示学习更具灵活性，潜表示更加规整。

Abstract: Deep learning has advanced time series forecasting, but periodicity drift, in which cycle timing and phase vary over time, remains a challenging problem. Existing methods predominantly model these sequences on fixed time grids, suffering from a limited ability to accommodate phase-related variation. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{POEM}, a phase-aware forecasting framework based on latent feature rotation using the special orthogonal group in two dimensions, denoted by $\mathrm{SO}(2)$. POEM aims to reduce the phase-related variability by learning a phase-correction coordinate and applying an invertible $\mathrm{SO}(2)$-based rotation to paired latent features. To extrapolate this correction coordinate, Directional Phase Increment Attention (DPIA) retrieves historical phase increments from similar temporal contexts and integrates them into future phase corrections. Experiments demonstrate that POEM achieves competitive performance, while qualitative visualizations suggest that the learned phase-aware transformation makes latent trajectories more regular.

</details>


### [104] [CausalOPD: First-Wrong-Step Supervision for Distilling Causal Chain Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03673)
*Jian Zhang,Bingyi Wang,Yizhi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CausalOPD框架，通过知识增强的教师提供因果规则约束下的轨迹，学生基于自身rollout分布进行在线过程蒸馏，采用因果阶段课程从证据级到机制级再到结论级逐步修复错误，显著提升路径正确率并降低"标签对但推理错"的比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键推理任务（如临床诊断、法律判决、工业故障诊断）需要步骤依赖的因果链，早期错误会传播且正确结论可能掩盖错误推理。大模型虽然表现优秀，但隐私、延迟和可控性需求促使将其蒸馏到本地部署的小模型。标准轨迹模仿无法纠正学生自身rollout分布上的过程错误，需要新的蒸馏方法。

Method: 提出CausalOPD（因果在线过程蒸馏）框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 知识增强教师：提供基于领域因果规则、实体关系和结构约束的轨迹；(2) 第一步错误定位与短视界强化学习修复：教师识别学生on-policy轨迹中最早验证违反约束的步骤，从已验证前缀出发用短视界RL局部修复；(3) 因果阶段课程：按证据级→机制级→结论级错误的传播顺序推进训练。

Result: 在三个领域上，CausalOPD相较序列级在线过程蒸馏将平均路径正确率提升23.4个百分点，并将"标签对但推理错"的比例从15.7%降至4.4%。领域特定的8B学生模型在路径正确率上全面超越两个商用参考模型。

Conclusion: 通过定位并修复学生rollout中的首个错误步骤，配合因果传播顺序的课程安排，能够有效蒸馏推理过程而非仅蒸馏最终答案，显著改善小模型在因果链推理任务上的路径正确性，并减少表面正确但推理无效的情况。

Abstract: Many critical reasoning tasks, including clinical diagnosis, legal judgment, and industrial fault diagnosis, require step-dependent causal chains in which early errors propagate and correct conclusions can mask invalid reasoning. Although large language models perform well on such tasks, privacy, latency, and controllability motivate distillation into locally deployable models. Standard trajectory imitation does not correct process errors on the student's own rollout distribution. We propose CausalOPD, a curriculum online process distillation framework. A knowledge-augmented teacher first provides trajectories grounded in domain-specific causal rules, entity relations, and structural constraints. The student then generates on-policy trajectories, and the teacher identifies the first wrong step, defined as the earliest transition that verifiably violates available constraints. Starting from the verified prefix, short-horizon reinforcement learning repairs this localized failure. A causal-stage curriculum advances from evidence-level to mechanism-level and conclusion-level errors, following their propagation order. Across three domains, CausalOPD improves average path correctness by 23.4 percentage points over sequence-level online process distillation and reduces the right-label-wrong-reasoning rate from 15.7% to 4.4%. The domain-specific 8B students also surpass both evaluated proprietary references in path correctness across all domains.

</details>


### [105] [DiagLoop: A Counterfactual Data Flywheel with Stage-Localized Reinforcement for Diagnostic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03674)
*Jian Zhang,Bingyi Wang,Yizhi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiagLoop是一种反事实数据飞轮机制，通过将物理关系或临床指南编码为训练监督，使诊断因果模型无需专家推理标注即可学习从证据到结论的推理路径。在8个工业系统和10个疾病类别上，8B模型分别比最强传统基线提高11.6和5.5个百分点，并超越商业参考模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诊断因果模型必须解释结论如何从证据中推导出来，因为诊断指导修复和治疗。然而，严重病例稀缺、记录中很少包含推理路径，且数据在不同配置间迁移效果差，增加了本地部署的难度。需要一种不依赖大量标注数据即可训练诊断推理能力的方法。

Method: DiagLoop采用反事实数据飞轮：(1) 训练阶段的教师模型通过变化原因、上下文和观测来提出反事实世界；(2) 独立混合检查器仅接受有效世界；(3) 学生模型执行症状抽象、因果链构建和根因归因；(4) 阶段特定标准识别最早失败点；(5) 对非终止失败进行有界修复以探测下游能力，弱项档案指导后续数据生成；(6) 阶段局部强化学习仅更新模型生成的延续部分，重放与保留减少遗忘；(7) 相同标准通过独立于提议者的检查来控制准入、归属、奖励和再生成。

Result: 仅使用合成场景且无任何案例级专家推理标注，8B模型在严格路径正确性上超越最强传统基线：8个工业系统提升11.6个百分点，10个疾病类别提升5.5个百分点；相比失序路由控制分别提升3.9和2.3个百分点。模型在两种领域均超越评估的商业参考模型，即使给予参考模型少样本示例或规格说明。

Conclusion: DiagLoop证明了通过反事实数据生成与独立验证的飞轮机制，可以在无需专家推理标注的情况下有效训练诊断因果推理模型，显著提升严格路径正确性，并超越商业参考模型，为稀缺领域的诊断推理提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Causal diagnostic models must explain how conclusions follow from evidence because diagnoses guide repairs and treatments. Yet serious cases are scarce, records rarely contain reasoning paths, and data transfer poorly across configurations, complicating local deployment. We present DiagLoop, a counterfactual data flywheel that converts codified physical relations or clinical guidelines, authored once per mechanism family, into training supervision beyond recorded cases. A training-only teacher proposes counterfactual worlds by varying causes, contexts, and observations, while an independent hybrid checker admits only valid worlds. The student reasons through symptom abstraction, causal-chain construction, and root-cause attribution. Stage-specific criteria identify its earliest failure. For nonterminal failures, a bounded repair probes downstream competence, and the resulting weakness profile guides subsequent data generation. Stage-localized reinforcement learning updates only the model-generated continuation, while replay and preservation reduce forgetting. The same criteria govern admission, attribution, reward, and regeneration through checks separate from the proposer. Using only synthesized scenarios and no case-level expert reasoning annotations, the resulting 8B model improves strict path correctness over the strongest conventional baseline. Gains are 11.6 points across eight industrial systems and 5.5 points across ten disease categories. Gains over a deranged-routing control are 3.9 and 2.3 points, respectively. The model also exceeds the evaluated proprietary references in both domains, even when they receive few-shot examples or the specification in context.

</details>


### [106] [LAEF: A Lead-Agnostic ECG Foundation Model Towards Point-of-Care Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03690)
*Edoardo Coppola,Stefano Fiorini,Pietro Liò,Mattia Savardi,Alberto Signoroni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LAEF（Lead-Agnostic ECG Foundation），一个7M参数的ECG基础模型，可原生处理任意导联子集（1-2导联）而无需零填充或架构修改。LAEF将ECG表示为可变大小的时空图，通过图注意力网络处理，在9.2M 12导联ECG上预训练，在18个下游数据集上达到或超越12倍规模的特化12导联基线，在资源受限场景下平均AUROC提升+3.2点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ECG基础模型架构上受限于固定的12导联输入，而实际点护理设备（如智能手表、手持ECG记录仪）通常只采集1-2个导联，导致现有模型在降级配置下性能退化或完全失效。

Method: 1）将ECG表示为可变大小的时空图，包含基于生理学驱动的导联内和导联间连接；2）使用图注意力网络（GAT）处理，可随激活导联数自然扩展；3）通过带随机导联采样的掩码节点建模预训练于920万个12导联ECG上。

Result: 在18个下游数据集中，全导联可用时LAEF与12倍参数量的特化12导联基线持平；在1-2导联点护理诊断场景下，使用单一随机采样导联在17/18个数据集中优于所有零填充替代方案，2导联时在14/18上胜出，平均AUROC增益+3.2点。在164种心血管疾病上的导联重要性研究显示，单一标准导联下群体水平性能稳定。

Conclusion: 架构层面的导联不可知性是LAEF优势的来源；LAEF在低导联配置下保持稳定性能，同时能恢复已确立的临床导联-疾病关联，适用于点护理ECG诊断场景。

Abstract: Point-of-care cardiac devices such as smartwatches and handheld ECG recorders typically capture 1--2 leads, yet existing ECG foundation models are architecturally constrained to fixed 12-lead inputs, degrading or failing under these reduced configurations. We introduce LAEF (Lead-Agnostic ECG Foundation), a 7M-parameter ECG foundation model that can natively process any lead subset without zero-padding or architectural modification. LAEF represents ECGs as variable-size spatiotemporal graphs with physiologically motivated intra- and inter-lead connectivity, processed by a Graph Attention Network that scales naturally with active lead count.Pre-trained on 9.2M 12-lead ECGs via masked node modelling with stochastic lead sampling, LAEF learns representations robust to lead configuration. Across 18 downstream datasets, LAEF is on par with specialized 12-lead baselines over 12$\times$ larger at full lead availability. Under direct point-of-care-oriented diagnostics (1--2 leads), it outperforms all zero-padded alternatives on 17 out of 18 datasets with with a single randomly sampled lead and on 14 out of 18 with 2 leads, with an average AUROC gain of +3.2 points. Representation analysis links this advantage to architectural lead-agnosticism, and a lead-importance study across 164 cardiovascular conditions shows population-level performance is stable across single standard input leads while still recovering established clinically lead-condition associations.

</details>


### [107] [Learning and Clustering on Temporal Graphs: Principles, Primitives, and Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03696)
*Nelson Aloysio Reis de Almeida Passos,Emanuele Carlini,Salvatore Trani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了时序图上的聚类问题，从原理（谱基础与可检测性阈值）、原语（GPU加速的谱聚类与多切片模块度优化）和池化（基于社区检测的粗粒度算子）三个视角，论证了传统算法方法在属性缺失时仍优于图神经网络，而神经模型仅在结构、时序与属性信号对齐时才更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管图神经网络在许多下游图任务上达到SOTA性能，但在聚类任务（尤其是效率与恢复精度方面）相对于传统的描述性和推断性聚类算法优势尚不明确。本文旨在厘清这一张力，并探讨何时应选用算法方法而非神经模型，同时为时序图提供可扩展的粗粒度（池化）解决方案。

Method: 通过三个相互关联的视角展开：(1) 原理——连接图学习与社区检测的谱基础及随机块模型（SBM）可检测性阈值；(2) 原语——利用GPU加速时序后端，使谱聚类和多切片模块度优化变得可追踪；(3) 池化——将有理论基础的社区检测视为时序图的粗粒度算子。

Result: 结果表明：在属性缺失或较弱的情况下，传统算法方法仍是合适工具，其瓶颈是可扩展性而非准确率；而当结构、时序和属性信号对齐时，神经模型最具优势。GPU加速原语使时序聚类可扩展，为理论基础的池化提供了路径。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个核心开放问题：基于社区的粗粒度化在何种条件下能够保留下游学习任务所需的动态信息？这为未来时序图表征学习提供了重要研究方向。

Abstract: This work focuses on the problem of learning on temporal graphs, with particular emphasis on the task of clustering: obtaining coarse-grained representations by aggregating information from nodes, edges, and temporal dynamics - a task related to pooling in machine learning on graphs, or community detection in network science. Although graph neural networks reach state-of-the-art performance across many downstream graph tasks, their advantage over established descriptive and inferential clustering algorithms is far less settled, especially under demands of efficiency and recovery accuracy. We frame this tension through three linked perspectives: principles, connecting graph learning and community detection through shared spectral foundations and detectability thresholds in stochastic block model regimes; primitives, making spectral clustering and multislice modularity optimization tractable through GPU-accelerated temporal backends; and pooling, viewing principled community detection as a theory-grounded coarse-graining operator for temporal graphs. Our results indicate that algorithmic methods remain the appropriate tool where attributes are absent or weak - scalability rather than accuracy being the binding obstacle - while neural models are most compelling when structural, temporal, and attribute signals align. By making temporal clustering scalable, GPU-accelerated primitives suggest a route toward theory-grounded pooling, while raising a central question: when does community-based coarse-graining preserve the dynamics needed for downstream learning tasks?

</details>


### [108] [To Describe or Construct Statistical Learning Models Using the Category-theoretical Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03706)
*Congwei Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一份面向初学者的统计学习综述报告，回顾了经典统计学习模型和知名算法，并从范畴论视角重新审视统计学习模型，旨在吸引包括基础数学在内的其他领域研究者参与统计学习研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计学习是机器学习和人工智能的主流方向，已产生大量可应用于现实问题的成果。然而，作者希望吸引来自其他领域（特别是基础数学）的研究者参与统计学习研究，因此需要一份系统总结经典模型和算法、并提供新的理论视角（范畴论）的综述性报告。

Method: 本文采用综述方式，系统总结经典统计学习模型和知名算法，适用于初学者；同时引入范畴论作为理解统计学习模型的新视角，通过范畴论框架重新诠释统计学习的理论结构。

Result: 本文梳理了经典统计学习模型与算法的核心内容，并成功从范畴论视角提供了对统计学习模型的抽象化理解框架，搭建了基础数学与统计学习之间的桥梁。

Conclusion: 通过范畴论视角重新审视统计学习，有助于吸引来自基础数学等领域的研究者参与统计学习相关研究，推动跨学科合作与新发展。

Abstract: Statistical learning is a fascinating field that has long been the mainstream of machine learning/artificial intelligence. A large number of results have been produced which can be widely applied to real-world problems. It also leads to many research topics and also stimulates new research. This report summarizes some classical statistical learning models and well-known algorithms, especially for amateurs, and provides a category-theoretic perspective on understanding statistical learning models. The aim is to attract researchers from other fields, including basic mathematics, to participate in the research related to statistical learning.

</details>


### [109] [Amortized Interventional Forecasting for Multivariate CIR Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03715)
*Andreas Sauter,Sumit Sourabh,Drona Kandhai,Erman Acar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CIR-ACTIVA框架，将轨迹视为时间戳观测，利用摊销模型实现无需逐场景重训的多时域分布因果效应估计，并结合因果多变量CIR数据生成过程提供配对观测/干预真实数据，以CDS利差为试验平台验证其因果选择性与时域校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CIR过程是金融中均值回复动态的标准模型，但其仅能刻画序列间相关（correlated）共动，无法回答外部冲击下的因果响应问题，观测条件分布会与历史共动混淆。

Method: 一是构建摊销模型进行分布因果效应估计，将轨迹视为时间戳观测，预测无需逐场景重训的多时域校准冲击响应；二是设计因果多变量CIR数据生成过程，提供市场无法获取的配对观测与干预真值。

Result: 在合成真值上验证CIR-ACTIVA的有效性，并通过回测生成轨迹对真实CDS数据做实践校准。相较观测与摊销因果推断基线，CIR-ACTIVA在联合分布的因果选择性和时域校准方面均领先，且干预律随时域变化时仍保持选择性，增益集中在短时域。

Conclusion: 该框架开启了一类针对耦合利差系统的what-if查询，包括CDS压力测试，这是观测型预测器无法回答的问题。

Abstract: Mean-reverting dynamics are pervasive in finance, and the Cox--Ingersoll--Ross (CIR) process is a standard model for the time series they produce, from short rates to credit default swap (CDS) spreads. Yet CIR models capture only \emph{correlated} co-movement, not \emph{causal} influence between series, so they cannot answer the system's response when one series is externally shocked, which observational conditionals confound with historical co-movement. We make two contributions. First, an amortized model for distributional causal effect estimation that frames trajectories as time-stamped observations and predicts the calibrated multi-horizon shock response without retraining per scenario. Second, a causal multivariate CIR data-generating process that supplies the paired observational and interventional ground truth that real markets cannot. We instantiate and calibrate the framework on CDS spreads as a testbed. CIR-ACTIVA's validity is established on synthetic ground truth, independent of how well the simulator matches reality, while practical grounding is assessed by backtesting the generated traces against real CDS data. Against observational and amortized causal-inference baselines, CIR-ACTIVA leads on both causal selectivity in the joint distribution and horizon-resolved calibration, retaining its selectivity once the interventional law varies over the horizon, with gains concentrating at short horizons. This opens up a class of what-if queries on coupled spread systems, CDS stress testing among them, that observational forecasters cannot answer.

</details>


### [110] [UNVaMP: Neural Knowledge Tracing with Variational Regularization of Latent Knowledge Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03811)
*Carson J. Cook,Ahmed J. Zerouali,Anthony Schmidt,Reginald Ziedzor,Paul Lin,Luke G. Eglington*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UNVaMP架构，一种集成学生-题目交互与内部记忆的知识追踪方法，生成学习者能力的动态潜在表征。纯神经配置（UNVaMP-MLP）在四个数据集的三个上取得最佳预测性能；混合配置（UNVaMP-MIRT）预测精度略逊但更具可解释性，其代价较小。该方法还支持波动性控制、不确定性量化和异构特征输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识追踪方法在预测准确性与可解释性之间存在权衡，同时缺乏对学习轨迹平滑度的显式控制以及对知识状态估计不确定性的量化。作者旨在提出一种统一的架构，兼具强大的预测能力与可解释性，同时提供对潜在变量波动的原则性控制机制。

Method: UNVaMP采用编码器-解码器架构，将学生-题目交互与内部记忆状态整合，生成随时间演化的潜在知识表征。支持两种配置：(1) 纯神经配置UNVaMP-MLP，用MLP做响应预测；(2) 混合配置UNVaMP-MIRT，用1PL MIRT测量函数在潜在空间上进行可解释的响应预测。通过显式约束控制学习轨迹的平滑度。

Result: UNVaMP-MLP在四个数据集中的三个上取得最佳预测性能；UNVaMP-MIRT的预测精度仅略低于MLP配置。实验数据表明辅助输入会引发UNVaMP-MIRT预测行为的结构化变化，表明其能感知超越响应正确性的底层结构。仿真实验显示UNVaMP在受控测量条件下能产生良好行为的知识状态估计。

Conclusion: UNVaMP兼具实用性与结构恢复能力，适合真实教育系统部署。混合配置中可解释性带来的预测代价是适度的，同时提供了波动性控制、不确定性量化和可解释知识状态估计等额外收益。

Abstract: We introduce the Unified Neural Variational Measurement of Proficiency (UNVaMP) architecture, a knowledge tracing method that integrates observed student-item interactions with internal memory to produce evolving latent representations of student knowledge. These representations support accurate predictions of future responses while enabling explicit control over the smoothness of estimated learning trajectories. UNVaMP can be configured as either a purely neural model or a hybrid model that predicts responses through an interpretable measurement function over the latent space. We show that a pure neural configuration (UNVaMP-MLP) achieves the strongest predictive performance among compared models on three out of four datasets. Meanwhile, a hybrid configuration (UNVaMP-MIRT, using a 1PL MIRT measurement function) lags only slightly behind UNVaMP-MLP, indicating that the predictive cost of interpretability is modest.
  Beyond predictive accuracy, UNVaMP provides the following: a principled mechanism for controlling volatility when estimating student latent variables, quantification of uncertainty over student knowledge state estimates, and flexible input specification that supports heterogeneous student-item interaction features. In addition, the hybrid UNVaMP-MIRT configuration generates interpretable moment-in-time student knowledge state estimates. Using an experimental dataset, we show that auxiliary inputs induce structured changes in the predictive behavior of UNVaMP-MIRT, consistent with sensitivity to underlying structure beyond response correctness. Furthermore, through a simulation study, we show that UNVaMP yields well-behaved knowledge state estimates under controlled measurement conditions. In total, these results indicate that UNVaMP is both useful for real-world education systems and capable of recovering underlying structure from student-item interactions.

</details>


### [111] [Resume Means Resume: A Machine-Checked Conformance Contract for Checkpoint, Interrupt, and Resume Semantics in Workflow Persistence Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03836)
*Sajjad Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RESUME CONTRACT，定义持久化API上六个性质（前缀延续、效应恰好一次、分支确定性、检查点有效性、一次性消费、恢复确定性）外加分支意图和活性义务；用TLA+模型在7.4百万状态下穷举验证参考语义；通过39单元的故障矩阵分离模型；并在五个主流代理工作流框架上测量，发现LangGraph、CrewAI、pydantic-graph均存在违反声明的行为，且无两个框架共享一致性画像。文章还提出REMIT参考序列器（Verus验证恢复核心与执行程序行级一致）修复分支和有效性单元，跨进程单元在读取路径修复，通过opt-in门在共享存储中声明消费来提供服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化执行状态框架在中断/崩溃/恢复时必须决定已触发效果的意义，但五个主流框架各有不同回答，没有一个暴露机器可检查的契约，且行为甚至违反其声明的片段。需要形式化契约和可测量的一致性标准。

Method: 定义了RESUME CONTRACT（六个性质+两个义务）；使用TLA+模型穷举检查参考语义（在扩展边界7.4M状态仍不变）；设计39单元故障矩阵作为分离模型手段；开发确定性、无LLM的测试工具在固定版本上测量各个框架；提出REMIT参考序列器并采用Verus形式化验证。

Result: LangGraph 1.2.9持久化记录第二个恢复值但从不查阅、静默持久化schema无效状态、真实SIGKILL后重执行持久化工作（跨中断恰好一次但跨崩溃至少一次）；CrewAI 1.15.2违反其书面声明重执行效应方法；pydantic-graph无法在中节点崩溃后恢复；无两框架共享一致性画像。消费一次在顺序下成立但在并发传递下失败（k个进程恢复一个驻停中断触发k次门控效应）；REMIT修复分支和有效性命，跨进程单元在读取路径修复并落地在opt-in门。

Conclusion: 现有代理工作流框架对恢复语义缺乏一致且可验证的契约，RESUME CONTRACT与REMIT提供了形式化规范与参考实现，证明了可修复性与可靠性差距的存在及具体修复路径。

Abstract: A framework that persists execution state so a run can be interrupted, survive a crash, and continue must decide what a resume means for effects that already fired. Five widely deployed agent workflow frameworks answer differently, none exposes a machine-checkable contract, and behavior violates even the fragments they state. The RESUME CONTRACT states six properties over the persistence API (prefix continuation, effect exactly-once, fork determinism, checkpoint validity, consume-once, recovery determinism), plus fork-intent and liveness obligations. A TLA+ model checks a reference semantics exhaustively, unchanged at scaled bounds (7.4 million states); a 39-cell fault matrix yields the separating models independence requires, and consume-once splits, its consumption clause independent of all six others. A deterministic, LLM-free harness measures them at pinned releases. LangGraph 1.2.9 durably records a second resume value and never consults it, persists schema-invalid state silently, and re-executes durably recorded work after a real SIGKILL: exactly-once across interrupts, at-least-once across crashes, on one API. CrewAI 1.15.2 re-executes completed effect-bearing methods against its written claim; pydantic-graph 1.x cannot resume after a mid-node crash; no two probed frameworks share a conformance profile. Consume-once holds sequentially and fails under concurrent delivery: k processes resuming one parked interrupt fire the gated effect k times, saturation 1.0 in 36 of 40 cells, and the failure crosses hosts. REMIT, a reference sequencer whose Verus-verified recovery core is line-identical to the shipped executable, repairs the fork and validity cells. The cross-process cell is repaired at the read path, and that repair ships: an opt-in gate claims consumption in the shared store, serving one racer and refusing the rest before any node executes.

</details>


### [112] [FedCritic-MIMO: Communication-Efficient Serverless Federated Critic Learning for Massive-MIMO Resource Control in Open and Disaggregated 6G RANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03852)
*Amin Farajzadeh,Melike Erol-Kantarci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCritic-MIMO，一个面向开放解聚6G RAN中自主部署的小区级控制器的通信高效的serverless联邦多智能体强化学习框架。控制器不共享训练器，保持本地actor和个性化critic组件，仅交换兼容的共享critic参数。在reuse-1多小区大规模MIMO OFDMA场景下，通过无线感知事件触发、自适应逐层top-k稀疏critic交换（含误差反馈）和均衡干扰感知融合，实现多小区协同调度、功率分配、波束赋形和干扰管理。实验表明其取得最佳性能-通信权衡，相比未压缩分布式critic交换减少76%通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G RAN向开放、解聚架构演进，小区级控制器需要独立部署并协同管理无线资源，但传统集中式多智能体强化学习需要中心化训练和轨迹收集，不适用于无参数服务器、无共享训练器的场景。同时，reuse-1大规模MIMO OFDMA系统中小区间干扰强耦合，如何在有限信号和跨控制器协作下实现高效资源控制是核心挑战。

Method: FedCritic-MIMO采用无服务器架构：每个基站本地执行actor并保留个性化critic组件，仅通过干扰感知图以点对点方式交换共享critic参数。方法包含三个关键技术：(1)无线感知事件触发机制控制何时触发交换；(2)自适应逐层top-k稀疏critic交换并配备误差反馈以压缩通信；(3)均衡干扰感知融合算法整合多方critic信息。理论分析对固定策略、冻结目标的critic回归给出条件有限时间平稳性和共识保证。

Result: 在强干扰耦合的reuse-1仿真中，FedCritic-MIMO在启发式、独立学习、集中训练和通信消融基线中取得最佳性能-通信权衡。它实现最高持出吞吐量、改善用户速率分布和平均SINR、提升QoS满意度，并在学习基线中达到每传输比特最低干扰成本。相对未压缩分布式critic交换，批评者通信开销降低76%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，无服务器的共享critic参数交换模式能够协调RAN控制器实现协同资源控制，无需中心化轨迹收集或参数服务器聚合，为开放解聚6G RAN中的多智能体强化学习提供了一条高效、可扩展且通信友好的实现路径。

Abstract: This paper proposes FedCritic-MIMO, a communication-efficient serverless federated multi-agent reinforcement learning framework for AI-native resource control across independently deployable cell-level controllers in open and disaggregated 6G RANs. Controllers share no trainer, retain local actors and personalized critic components, and exchange only compatible shared critic parameters. FedCritic-MIMO targets reuse-$1$ multi-cell massive-MIMO OFDMA deployments, where RAN controllers jointly manage user scheduling, per-stream power allocation, beamforming, interference, and long-term QoS with limited inter-controller signaling. Each base station locally executes its actor without centralized training or actor federation, while critic knowledge is exchanged peer-to-peer over an interference-aware graph. It enables this collaboration through wireless-aware event triggering, adaptive layer-wise top-$k$ sparse critic exchange with error feedback, and balanced interference-aware fusion. We establish conditional finite-time stationarity and consensus guarantees for the balanced, compressed peer-to-peer critic recursion under a fixed-policy, frozen-target critic-regression model. In strongly interference-coupled reuse-$1$ simulations, FedCritic-MIMO achieves the best performance-communication tradeoff among heuristic, independent-learning, centralized-training, and communication-ablation baselines. It achieves the highest held-out throughput, improves user-rate distribution and mean SINR, increases QoS satisfaction, and attains the lowest interference cost per delivered bit among learning baselines. It reduces critic-communication overhead by $76\%$ relative to uncompressed distributed critic exchange. These results demonstrate that serverless exchange of compatible shared critic parameters can coordinate RAN controllers without centralized trajectory collection or parameter-server aggregation.

</details>


### [113] [Quantization Effects on Biomedical LLM Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03854)
*Anton Rasmussen,Hong Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了Mistral-7B系列模型（Base、BioMistral、Instruct）在PubMed RCT句子分类任务上的表现，发现概率提取协议（即评分规则）是影响标定（calibration）的首要因素：从求和改为均值token对数似然评分会反转模型间的标定排名。提示模板对准确率的影响（7-24个百分点）达到甚至超过模型差异，而量化精度的影响相对较小且有异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解码器语言模型用作分类器时，其预测类别概率高度依赖实现选择（如提示模板、verbalizer、评分规则），但这些实现细节很少被视为实验变量。这导致不同研究之间的比较缺乏公平性，且模型的真实标定性能可能被掩盖。作者希望通过受控实验揭示这些看似"实现细节"的因素对模型评估结果的首要影响。

Method: 在PubMed RCT句子分类任务（n=2000）上对三种Mistral-7B变体（Base、BioMistral、Instruct）进行受控评估。实验跨FP16、INT8、INT4三种精度，使用四种答案文本提示模板，系统性地变化评分规则（求和vs均值token对数似然评分）及verbalizer映射，并配合温度缩放和PubMedBERT参考模型进行对比分析。

Result: 1) 评分规则是标定的首要驱动因素：从求和到均值评分使BioMistral的ECE从0.097升至0.289，而Instruct从0.237降至0.096，准确率变化小于1个百分点（专用模型）或4-6个百分点（基础模型）；2) 提示模板导致7-24个百分点的准确率差异，超过模型级效应；3) INT8量化对BioMistral和Instruct影响仅1-2个百分点，但基础模型在某些模板上出现高达+4.2个百分点的影响；4) INT4量化产生异构但非灾难性的效果；5) 温度缩放仅在求和评分规则下有效降低ECE。

Conclusion: 提示模板设计和评分归一化是评估解码器语言模型标定性能时的一阶实验决策，其影响可与模型本身的选择相提并论甚至更大。未来在报告LLM分类器性能时，必须将评分规则和模板纳入核心实验变量，而非视为可忽略的实现细节。

Abstract: When decoder language models are used as classifiers, predicted class probabilities depend on implementation choices, including the prompt template, verbalizer (label-to-token mapping), and scoring rule, that are rarely treated as experimental variables. We present a controlled evaluation of three Mistral-7B variants (Base, BioMistral, and Instruct) on PubMed RCT sentence classification (n=2000) under FP16, INT8, and INT4 precision using four answer-text prompt templates. Our primary finding is that the probability extraction protocol dominates apparent calibration. Switching from summed to mean token log-likelihood scoring reverses the calibration ranking between models: BioMistral average expected calibration error increases from 0.097 to 0.289, whereas Instruct decreases from 0.237 to 0.096, while accuracy changes by less than 1 percentage point for the specialized models but 4-6 percentage points for the base model. Prompt template choice produces accuracy differences of 7-24 percentage points, comparable to or larger than model-level effects. On one template, BioMistral outperforms Instruct although the overall mean favors Instruct by only 1.3 percentage points. For BioMistral and Instruct, INT8 quantization changes accuracy and F1 by only 1-2 percentage points relative to FP16, whereas the base model shows larger INT8 effects on some templates (up to +4.2 percentage points).
